
	/**/
		DECLARE @ResultCheckDuplicateForCmpNationalCode INT
		EXEC MobspCheckDuplicateCmpNationalCode @cmpNationalCode,0,@LanguageID,@DefaultLanguageID /*AI14010329*/
		SET @ResultCheckDuplicateForCmpNationalCode = @@ROWCOUNT
		IF(@ResultCheckDuplicateForCmpNationalCode > 0)
			SET @IsDuplicate = 1
	/**/
	END
END
/*AI990730- check cmpEconomicCode duplicate*/
IF(@cmpEconomicCode IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	IF(@CantSaveDupCmpEconomicCode= 1)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		DECLARE @ResultCheckDuplicateForCmpEconomicCode INT
		EXEC MobspCheckDuplicateCmpEconomicCode @cmpEconomicCode,0,@LanguageID,@DefaultLanguageID /*AI14010329*/
		SET @ResultCheckDuplicateForCmpEconomicCode = @@ROWCOUNT
		IF(@ResultCheckDuplicateForCmpEconomicCode > 0)
			SET @IsDuplicate = 1
	/**/
	END
END
/**/
/*AI990730- check cmpEmail duplicate*/
IF(@cmpEmail IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @ResultCheckDuplicateForCmpEmail INT
	EXEC MobspCheckDuplicateCmpEmail @cmpEmail,0,@LanguageID,@DefaultLanguageID /*AI14010329*/
	SET @ResultCheckDuplicateForCmpEmail = @@ROWCOUNT
	IF(@ResultCheckDuplicateForCmpEmail > 0)
		SET @IsDuplicate = 1
END

IF(@cmpMobile IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @ResultCheckDuplicateForCmpMobile INT
	EXEC MobspCheckDuplicateCmpMobile @cmpMobile,0,@LanguageID,@DefaultLanguageID /*AI14010329*/
	SET @ResultCheckDuplicateForCmpMobile = @@ROWCOUNT
	IF(@ResultCheckDuplicateForCmpMobile > 0)
		SET @IsDuplicate = 1
END
/**/
/*AI990818 - cmpDraft check Added*/
IF(@cmpIsDraft IS NOT NULL AND @cmpIsDraft = 1)
BEGIN
	IF EXISTS (
	SELECT * FROM dbo.CrmsysRollPermission
	WHERE FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmCompanyI' )
	AND FormActionId = (SELECT Id FROM dbo.CrmsysFormActions WHERE ActionName = N'actCanSaveCmpIsDraft'
						AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmCompanyI' ))
	AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @cmpDraft INT
		DECLARE @MaxNumberCmpDraft INT
		EXEC @cmpDraft = [dbo].[MobspReturnCmpDraftCount] @UserId

		SELECT @MaxNumberCmpDraft =
		ISNULL((SELECT MaxNumberCmpDraft 
				FROM [dbo].[Users] U 
				WHERE U.UserId = 3),0)
	
		IF(@cmpDraft >= @MaxNumberCmpDraft AND @MaxNumberCmpDraft > 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=CmpIsDraftAccess|' +  CAST(@MaxNumberCmpDraft AS NVARCHAR(10))  + '|Msg=CmpIsDraftAccess|'
		END
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=CmpIsDraftNotAccess|' + 'NoAccess' +'|Msg=CmpIsDraftNotAccess|'
	END
END
IF (@@ERROR > 0)
BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Input Company' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
ELSE 
COMMIT TRAN

						IF(@CantSaveDupCmpMainPrsNationalCode = 1 AND ISNULL(@cmpKind, 0) = 1)
						BEGIN
							IF(@cmpMainPersonId IS NOT NULL)
							BEGIN
								IF(@CompanyPersonID = @cmpMainPersonId)
								BEGIN
									DECLARE @ResultCheckDuplicateForMainPrsNationalCode INT
									EXEC MobspCheckDuplicateCmpMainPrsNationalCodeByCmpKind @prsNationalCode ,0 , 1, @LanguageID, @DefaultLanguageID
									SET @ResultCheckDuplicateForMainPrsNationalCode = @@ROWCOUNT
									IF(@ResultCheckDuplicateForMainPrsNationalCode > 0)
										SET @IsDuplicate = 1
								END
							END
						END
					END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsIdentityNumber'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsIdentityNumber = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsFatherName'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsFatherName = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsBirthDate'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsBirthDate = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE REPLACE(@PrsFieldValue, '/', '') END	
					/*RZ961206- Add time to date*/						
					SET @prsBirthDateTime = CASE WHEN @prsBirthDate IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@prsBirthDate, NULL/*AI14010122 - @RegisterTime*/) END			 	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsBirthPlace'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsBirthPlace = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsIsMarried'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsIsMarried = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL 
										WHEN @PrsFieldValue = 'false' THEN 0 
										ELSE 1 END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsMarriageDate'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsMarriageDate = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE REPLACE(@PrsFieldValue, '/', '') END
					/*RZ961206- Add time to date*/							
					SET @prsMarriageDateTime = CASE WHEN @prsMarriageDate IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@prsMarriageDate, NULL/*AI14010122 - @RegisterTime*/) END			 	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsEducationField'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsEducationField = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsSkill'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsSkill = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsStudyField'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsStudyField = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsInteresting'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsInteresting = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsSpecialPoint'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsSpecialPoint = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsDescription'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsDescription = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsImportDate'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsImportDate = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE REPLACE(@PrsFieldValue, '/', '') END	
					/*RZ961206- Add time to date*/							
					SET @prsImportDateTime = CASE WHEN @prsImportDate IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@prsImportDate, @RegisterTime) END			 	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsFirstRegisterUserId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @prsFirstRegisterUserId = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PrsFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsDepartmentId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @prsDepartmentId = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PrsFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsStopSendSms'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsStopSendSms = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL 
										WHEN @PrsFieldValue = 'false' THEN 0 
										ELSE 1 END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsOrderView'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @prsOrderView = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PrsFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsWorkingOut'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsWorkingOut = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL 
										WHEN @PrsFieldValue = 'false' THEN 0 
										ELSE 1 END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'CompanyPersonTemplateID'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @CompanyPersonTemplateID = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PrsFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsFirstRegisterDatePersian'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsFirstRegisterDatePersian = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE REPLACE(@PrsFieldValue, '/', '') END							
					/*RZ961206- Add time to date*/	
					SET @prsFirstRegisterDate = CASE WHEN @prsFirstRegisterDatePersian IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@prsFirstRegisterDatePersian, @RegisterTime) END			 	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				/*IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsTelegramId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @prsTelegramId = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PrsFieldValue AS INT) END
				END	*/
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'PersonPicture'
				BEGIN
					SET @PersonPicture = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PrsFieldValue AS VARBINARY(MAX)) END
					/*RZ961017- convert image*/
					SET @PersonPicture = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE 
						(SELECT CAST(N'' AS xml).value('xs:base64Binary(sql:variable("@PrsFieldValue"))', 'varbinary(max)'))
					END														
				END							
				/*END PERSON*/		
				/*END GENERAL*/
				/*---------------------------- END Fill variabele --------------------------------*/
				/*---------------------------- Remove current parameter from listparam ------------*/
				SET @PAllFields = SUBSTRING(@PAllFields,
                                  LEN(@PrsField + '|#|') + 1,
                                  LEN(@PAllFields))                                                                                                      
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				SET @PrsField = @PAllFields
				SET @PAllFields = NULL
				/*SELECT @CmpField*/
			END
		END  
		/************* Insert to tmp table***************/
		INSERT INTO @PersonTmpTable 
				 (
					[CompanyPersonID],
					[prsCompanyId],
					[prsLName],
					[prsFName],
					[prsTitleId],
					[prsJobId],
					[prsTypeId],
					[prsGroupId],
					[prsSexId],
					[prsEducationId],
					[prsPreTel],
					[prsTel],
					[prsTelUpTo],
					[prsTelDesc],
					[prsTelII],
					[prsFax],
					[prsFaxDesc],
					[prsMobile],
					[prsWebSite],
					[prsEmail],
					[prsEmailII],
					[prsCountryId],
					[prsProvinceId],
					[prsCityId],
					[prsAddress],
					[prsAddressDesc],
					[prsZipCode],
					[prsPostBox],
					[prsNationalCode],
					[prsIdentityNumber],
					[prsFatherName],
					[prsBirthDate],
					[prsBirthDateTime],
					[prsBirthPlace],
					[prsIsMarried],
					[prsMarriageDate],
					[prsMarriageDateTime],
					[prsEducationField],
					[prsSkill],
					[prsStudyField],
					[prsInteresting],
					[prsSpecialPoint],
					[prsDescription],
					[prsImportDate],
					[prsImportDateTime],
					[prsDepartmentId],
					[prsStopSendSms],
					[prsOrderView],
					[prsWorkingOut],
					[CompanyPersonTemplateID],
					[prsFirstRegisterUserId],
					[prsFirstRegisterDatePersian],
					[prsTelegramId],
					[prsFirstRegisterDate],
					[PersonPicture]
				)
				VALUES
				(
					@CompanyPersonID,
					@prsCompanyId,
					@prsLName,
					@prsFName,
					@prsTitleId,
					@prsJobId,
					@prsTypeId,
					@prsGroupId,
					@prsSexId,
					@prsEducationId,
					@prsPreTel,
					@prsTel,
					@prsTelUpTo,
					@prsTelDesc,
					@prsTelII,
					@prsFax,
					@prsFaxDesc,
					@prsMobile,
					@prsWebSite,
					@prsEmail,
					@prsEmailII,
					@prsCountryId,
					@prsProvinceId,
					@prsCityId,
					@prsAddress,
					@prsAddressDesc,
					@prsZipCode,
					@prsPostBox,
					@prsNationalCode,
					@prsIdentityNumber,
					@prsFatherName,
					@prsBirthDate,
					@prsBirthDateTime,
					@prsBirthPlace,
					@prsIsMarried,
					@prsMarriageDate,
					@prsMarriageDateTime,
					@prsEducationField,
					@prsSkill,
					@prsStudyField,
					@prsInteresting,
					@prsSpecialPoint,
					@prsDescription,
					@prsImportDate,
					@prsImportDateTime,
					@prsDepartmentId,
					@prsStopSendSms,
					@prsOrderView,
					@prsWorkingOut,
					@CompanyPersonTemplateID,
					@prsFirstRegisterUserId,
					@prsFirstRegisterDatePersian,
					@prsTelegramId,
					@prsFirstRegisterDate,
					@PersonPicture
				)                                              
		/*END PERSON*/		
        /*END GENERAL*/
        /*---------------------------- END Fill fields ------------------------------------*/
        /*---------------------------- Remove current parameter from listparam ------------*/
        SET @PersonList = SUBSTRING(@PersonList,
                                  LEN(@PrsAllFields + '|^#^|') + 1,
                                  LEN(@PersonList))                                  
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SET @PrsAllFields = @PersonList
        SET @PersonList = NULL
        /*SELECT @CmpField*/
    END
END

        WHILE LEN(@CnAllFields) > 0
		BEGIN

			BEGIN
				SET @CnvsField = SUBSTRING(@CnAllFields,
											0,
											PATINDEX('%|#|%', @CnAllFields))
				/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
				/*Get parameter name and split value form parameter*/
				/*For example => cnvsTopicId=102 => cnvsTopicId= => cnvsTopicId => Compare => value*/
				DECLARE @CnvsFieldValue NVARCHAR(MAX)
				DECLARE @CnvsFieldName NVARCHAR(200)
				SET @CnvsFieldName = SUBSTRING(@CnvsField, 1, CHARINDEX('=',@CnvsField)-1)                                    
				SET @CnvsFieldValue = SUBSTRING(@CnvsField, CHARINDEX('=', @CnvsField)+1, Len(@CnvsField))

/****************************** CompanyAddress **********************************/
/********************************************************************************/
/*AI14021009*/
BEGIN TRAN
/**/
DECLARE @CmpAddAllFields NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
DECLARE @CmpAddField NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
/**************** Define & set null field **************/
DECLARE 
	@CompanyAddressId INT = NULL,
	@cmpaddCompanyId INT = NULL,
	@cmpaddCityId INT = NULL,
	@CompanyAddress NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
	@cmpaddDesc NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
	@cmpaddZipCode NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@cmpaddPostBox NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@cmpaddCoordinateLatitude NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
	@cmpaddCoordinateLongitude NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
	@cmpaddCoordinateLastDate DATETIME = NULL,
	@cmpaddCoordinateLastDatePersian VARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@cmpaddCoordinateZoomLevel INT = NULL,
	@cmpaddCoordinateLastUserId INT = NULL,
	@cmpaddTypeId INT = NULL

WHILE (@CompanyAddressList IS NOT NULL) AND LEN(@CompanyAddressList) > 0
BEGIN
	/*@CompanyAddressList = N'cmpaddCompanyId=123|&|cmpaddCityId=321|&||^#^|cmpaddCompanyId=desc2|&|cmpaddCityId=2|&||^#^|'*/
    IF PATINDEX('%|^#^|%', @CompanyAddressList) > 0
    BEGIN
        SET @CmpAddAllFields = SUBSTRING(@CompanyAddressList,
                                    0,
                                    PATINDEX('%|^#^|%', @CompanyAddressList))
        /*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
        /*For example => @CmpAddAllFields = cmpaddCompanyId=123|&|cmpaddCityId=321|&|*/
        /*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
        /*---------------------------- BEGIN Fill fields  --------------------------------*/
        /*BEGIN Company Address*/
        DECLARE @CAAllFields NVARCHAR(MAX)
        SET @CAAllFields = @CmpAddAllFields
        
        /**************** set null field **************/
        SET @CompanyAddressId  = NULL
		SET @cmpaddCompanyId = NULL
		SET @cmpaddCityId = NULL
		SET @CompanyAddress = NULL
		SET @cmpaddDesc = NULL
		SET @cmpaddZipCode = NULL
		SET @cmpaddPostBox = NULL
		SET @cmpaddCoordinateLatitude = NULL
		SET @cmpaddCoordinateLongitude = NULL
		SET @cmpaddCoordinateLastDate = NULL
		SET @cmpaddCoordinateLastDatePersian = NULL
		SET @cmpaddCoordinateZoomLevel = NULL
		SET @cmpaddCoordinateLastUserId = NULL
		SET @cmpaddTypeId = NULL
        /**/
        WHILE LEN(@CAAllFields) > 0
		BEGIN
			IF PATINDEX('%|#|%', @CAAllFields) > 0
			BEGIN
				SET @CmpAddField = SUBSTRING(@CAAllFields,
											0,
											PATINDEX('%|#|%', @CAAllFields))
				/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
				/*Get parameter name and split value form parameter*/
				/*For example => cmpaddCompanyId=102 => cmpaddCompanyId= => cmpaddCompanyId => Compare => value*/
				DECLARE @CmpAddFieldValue NVARCHAR(MAX)
				DECLARE @CmpAddFieldName NVARCHAR(200)
				SET @CmpAddFieldName = SUBSTRING(@CmpAddField, 1, CHARINDEX('=',@CmpAddField)-1)                                    
				SET @CmpAddFieldValue = SUBSTRING(@CmpAddField, CHARINDEX('=', @CmpAddField)+1, Len(@CmpAddField))
				/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
				/*---------------------------- BEGIN Fill variabele ------------------------------*/
				/*BEGIN Conversation*/
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @CmpAddFieldName = 'CompanyAddressId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @CompanyAddressId = CASE WHEN @CmpAddFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CmpAddFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @CmpAddFieldName = 'cmpaddCompanyId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @cmpaddCompanyId = CASE WHEN @CmpAddFieldValue  = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CmpAddFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @CmpAddFieldName = 'cmpaddCityId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @cmpaddCityId = CASE WHEN @CmpAddFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CmpAddFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @CmpAddFieldName = 'CompanyAddress'
				BEGIN
					SET @CompanyAddress = CASE WHEN @CmpAddFieldValue = '' OR @CmpAddFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @CmpAddFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @CmpAddFieldName = 'cmpaddDesc'
				BEGIN
					SET @cmpaddDesc = CASE WHEN @CmpAddFieldValue = '' OR @CmpAddFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @CmpAddFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @CmpAddFieldName = 'cmpaddZipCode'
				BEGIN
					SET @cmpaddZipCode = CASE WHEN @CmpAddFieldValue = '' OR @CmpAddFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @CmpAddFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @CmpAddFieldName = 'cmpaddPostBox'
				BEGIN
					SET @cmpaddPostBox = CASE WHEN @CmpAddFieldValue = '' OR @CmpAddFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @CmpAddFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @CmpAddFieldName = 'cmpaddCoordinateLatitude'
				BEGIN
					SET @cmpaddCoordinateLatitude = CASE WHEN @CmpAddFieldValue = '' OR @CmpAddFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @CmpAddFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @CmpAddFieldName = 'cmpaddCoordinateLongitude'
				BEGIN
					SET @cmpaddCoordinateLongitude = CASE WHEN @CmpAddFieldValue = '' OR @CmpAddFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @CmpAddFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @CmpAddFieldName = 'cmpaddCoordinateZoomLevel'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @cmpaddCoordinateZoomLevel = CASE WHEN @CmpAddFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CmpAddFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @CmpAddFieldName = 'cmpaddTypeId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @cmpaddTypeId = CASE WHEN @CmpAddFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CmpAddFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				/*END Conversation*/		
				/*END GENERAL*/
				/*---------------------------- END Fill variabele --------------------------------*/
				/*---------------------------- Remove current parameter from listparam ------------*/
				SET @CAAllFields = SUBSTRING(@CAAllFields,
                                  LEN(@CmpAddField + '|#|') + 1,
                                  LEN(@CAAllFields))                                                                                                      
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				SET @CmpAddField = @CAAllFields
				SET @CAAllFields = NULL
				
			END
		END  
		/************* Insert to tmp table***************/
		IF(ISNULL(@cmpaddCoordinateLatitude, '') <> '' AND 
				ISNULL(@cmpaddCoordinateLongitude, '') <> '')
		BEGIN
			SET @cmpaddCoordinateLastDate = @RegisterDate
			SET @cmpaddCoordinateLastDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian
			SET @cmpaddCoordinateLastUserId = @UserId
		END
		/**/
		INSERT INTO @CompanyAddressTmpTable 
			(
				[CompanyAddressId] ,
				[cmpaddCompanyId] ,
				[cmpaddCityId] ,
				[CompanyAddress] ,
				[cmpaddDesc] ,
				[cmpaddZipCode] ,
				[cmpaddPostBox] ,
				[cmpaddCoordinateLatitude] ,
				[cmpaddCoordinateLongitude] ,
				[cmpaddCoordinateLastDate] ,
				[cmpaddCoordinateLastDatePersian] ,
				[cmpaddCoordinateZoomLevel] ,
				[cmpaddCoordinateLastUserId] ,
				[cmpaddTypeId]
			)
			VALUES
			(
				@CompanyAddressId ,
				@cmpaddCompanyId ,
				@cmpaddCityId ,
				@CompanyAddress ,
				@cmpaddDesc ,
				@cmpaddZipCode ,
				@cmpaddPostBox ,
				@cmpaddCoordinateLatitude ,
				@cmpaddCoordinateLongitude ,
				@cmpaddCoordinateLastDate ,
				@cmpaddCoordinateLastDatePersian ,
				@cmpaddCoordinateZoomLevel ,
				@cmpaddCoordinateLastUserId ,
				@cmpaddTypeId
			)					                      
		/*END Conversation*/		
        /*END GENERAL*/
        /*---------------------------- END Fill fields ------------------------------------*/
        /*---------------------------- Remove current parameter from listparam ------------*/
        SET @CompanyAddressList = SUBSTRING(@CompanyAddressList,
                                  LEN(@CmpAddAllFields + '|^#^|') + 1,
                                  LEN(@CompanyAddressList))                                  
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SET @CmpAddAllFields = @CompanyAddressList
        SET @CompanyAddressList = NULL
        
    END
END
/**/
IF (@@ERROR > 0)
BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Input CompanyAddress' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
ELSE 
COMMIT TRAN

					   SET [cmpMainPersonId] = (SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyPersonID] FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons WHERE prsCompanyId = CompanyId ORDER BY ISNULL(prsOrderView, CompanyPersonID)) 	
					WHERE [cmpMainPersonId] IS NULL
					AND [CompanyID] = @InsertCmpId
					/*AI14000929-end*/
					/*************** Ids inserted ********/				
					/*SET @InsertedPersonIds = @InsertedPersonIds  + CAST(@InsertPrsId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '|'*/
					SET @InsertedPrsIdsInMobAndDb = @InsertedPrsIdsInMobAndDb + CAST(@CompanyPersonID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ',' + CAST(@InsertPrsId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '|'
					SET @InsertedPersonIds = @InsertedPrsIdsInMobAndDb
				END
				ELSE /*when companyid <> @prsCompanyId that come from mobile*/
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '<PRSID>' + CAST(@CompanyPersonID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '<\PRSID>' + 'Msg=1'
				
				
			/**/					 					
			FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrPrs INTO @CompanyPersonID, @prsCompanyId, @prsLName, @prsFName, @prsTitleId, @prsJobId, @prsTypeId, @prsGroupId,
										 @prsSexId, @prsEducationId, @prsPreTel, @prsTel, @prsTelUpTo, @prsTelDesc, @prsTelII, @prsFax, @prsFaxDesc,
										 @prsMobile, @prsWebSite, @prsEmail, @prsEmailII, @prsCountryId, @prsProvinceId, @prsCityId, @prsAddress,
										 @prsAddressDesc, @prsZipCode, @prsPostBox, @prsNationalCode, @prsIdentityNumber, @prsFatherName, @prsBirthDate,
										 @prsBirthDateTime, @prsBirthPlace, @prsIsMarried, @prsMarriageDate, @prsMarriageDateTime, @prsEducationField,
										 @prsSkill, @prsStudyField, @prsInteresting, @prsSpecialPoint, @prsDescription, @prsImportDate, @prsImportDateTime,
										 @prsFirstRegisterUserId, @prsDepartmentId, @prsStopSendSms, @prsOrderView, @prsWorkingOut, @CompanyPersonTemplateID,
										 @prsFirstRegisterUserId, @prsFirstRegisterDatePersian, @prsTelegramId, @prsFirstRegisterDate, @PersonPicture		
			END
			CLOSE CrsrPrs
			DEALLOCATE CrsrPrs    					 	 	 
	END
	END
END
ELSE /*Not insert company*/
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=0' 	
/**/
IF (@@ERROR > 0)
BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert Person' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
ELSE 
COMMIT TRAN 
/**/

/****************************** Insert Projects *********************************/

BEGIN TRAN
/**/
/*check company is inserted*/
IF(@InsertedCompanyId > 0)
BEGIN
	IF (@ProjectList IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
		/*RZ961006- Remove insert in tmpTables*/
		IF(@IsDuplicate = 0) /* @IsDuplicate = 0 ====> insert to main table*/
		BEGIN
			DECLARE CrsrPrj Cursor For
					SELECT 
						[ProjectID], [prjCompanyId], [prjTitle], [prjDesc], [prjStartDate], [prjStartDay], [prjStartTime],
						[prjStartDateTime], [prjStarterUserId], [prjFinishedStatusId], [prjIsFinished], [prjFinishedDate],
						[prjFinishedDay], [prjFinishedTime], [prjFinishedDateTime], [prjFinishedUserId], [prjGradeId],
						[prjPriorityId], [prjFirstRegisterUserId], [prjGroupId], [prjcnvsStatusGroupId], [prjOrderView], [prjPostFix],
						[prjFirstRegisterDatePersian], [prjFirstRegisterDate], [prjRemindDate], [prjRemindDateTime], [prjInsertForbidden],
						/*RZ971103- Add field*/ /*RZ971103- Add field*/	/*AI14000131 - Add*/
						[prjFOCmpCanSee], [prjIsTicket], [prjUserDepartmentId],[prjHaveSms],[prjSmsPrsIds],[prjSmsText],
						[prjSmsSendDate] ,[prjSmsSendDatePersian],[prjInquiryNum] ,[prjSubject] ,[prjTitleGroup] ,[prjCategoryId] ,
						[prjDescI] ,[prjNumRef] ,[prjNumR]
						/*AI14010317*/, [prjToDoDateTime], [prjToDoRegisterDateTime], [prjToDoDoneDateTime], [prjFOCmpCanInsCnvs], [prjFOCmpCanNotInsCnvs], [prjTicketStop], [prjIsTicketNew]  /*AI14010317*/
						,[prjToDoTimeSec] /*AI14010412*/ , [prjToDoTimeRegisterHistory] /*AI14010416*/, [prjcmpPresentationId], [prjcmpCampaignIds], /*AI14010728*/
						[prjcmpQuickRegisterDefSettingId] /*AI14020504*/

					FROM @ProjectTmpTable		
				/**/
				OPEN CrsrPrj
				FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrPrj INTO @ProjectID, @prjCompanyId, @prjTitle, @prjDesc, @prjStartDate, @prjStartDay, @prjStartTime,
						@prjStartDateTime, @prjStarterUserId, @prjFinishedStatusId, @prjIsFinished, @prjFinishedDate,
						@prjFinishedDay, @prjFinishedTime, @prjFinishedDateTime, @prjFinishedUserId, @prjGradeId,
						@prjPriorityId, @prjFirstRegisterUserId, @prjGroupId, @prjcnvsStatusGroupId, @prjOrderView, @prjPostFix,
						@prjFirstRegisterDatePersian, @prjFirstRegisterDate, @prjRemindDate, @prjRemindDateTime, @prjInsertForbidden,
						/*RZ971103- Add field*/ /*RZ971103- Add field*/	/*AI14000131 - Add*/
						@prjFOCmpCanSee, @prjIsTicket, @prjUserDepartmentId,@prjHaveSms,@prjSmsPrsIds,@prjSmsText ,@prjSmsSendDate ,
						@prjSmsSendDatePersian ,@prjInquiryNum,@prjSubject,@prjTitleGroup,@prjCategoryId,@prjDescI,@prjNumRef,@prjNumR
						/*AI14010317*/,@prjToDoDateTime, @prjToDoRegisterDateTime, @prjToDoDoneDateTime, @prjFOCmpCanInsCnvs, @prjFOCmpCanNotInsCnvs, @prjTicketStop, @prjIsTicketNew /*AI14010317*/
						,@prjToDoTimeSec /*AI14010412*/, @prjToDoTimeRegisterHistory /*AI14010416*/, @prjcmpPresentationId, @prjcmpCampaignIds, /*AI14010728*/
						@prjcmpQuickRegisterDefSettingId /*AI14020504*/
				WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
				BEGIN
				/**/
					IF(@prjCompanyId = @CompanyID)
					BEGIN
						/*AI14000201 - Add duplicate checks*/
						/**************NumRef Check Dup****************/
						IF EXISTS (
						SELECT * FROM dbo.CrmsysRollPermission
						WHERE FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmDefineProjects' )
						AND FormActionId = (SELECT Id FROM dbo.CrmsysFormActions WHERE ActionName = N'actAutoCalcNumRef'
											AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmDefineProjects' ))
						AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
						BEGIN
							EXEC dbo.MobspReturnProjectNumRef
								@prjCompanyId = @InsertCmpId,
								@prjInquiryNum = @prjInquiryNum,
								@prjNumR = @prjNumR,
								@prjStarterUserId = @prjStarterUserId,
								@languageID = @LanguageID,
								@defaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID,
								@selectedNumRef = @prjNumRef OUTPUT
						END

						IF(ISNULL(@prjNumRef, '') <> '')
						BEGIN
							IF EXISTS (
							SELECT * FROM dbo.CrmsysRollPermission
							WHERE FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmDefineProjects' )
							AND FormActionId = (SELECT Id FROM dbo.CrmsysFormActions WHERE ActionName = N'actNotDupNumRef'
												AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmDefineProjects' ))
							AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
							BEGIN
								EXEC dbo.MobspCheckDuplicatePrjNumRef
									@prjNumRef = @prjNumRef,
									@CurrId = 0,
									@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
									@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID

								IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
								BEGIN
									SET @IsDuplicate = 1
								END
							END
						END
						/**************PrjDesc Check Dup****************/
						IF(ISNULL(@PrjDesc, '') <> '')
						BEGIN
							IF EXISTS (
							SELECT * FROM dbo.CrmsysRollPermission
							WHERE FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmDefineProjects' )
							AND FormActionId = (SELECT Id FROM dbo.CrmsysFormActions WHERE ActionName = N'actNotCanSavePrjDescDup'
												AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmDefineProjects' ))
							AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
							BEGIN
								EXEC [MobspCheckDuplicatePrjDesc]
									@PrjDesc = @PrjDesc,
									@CurrId = 0,
									@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
									@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID

								IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
								BEGIN
									SET @IsDuplicate = 1
								END
							END
						END
						/**************PostFix Check Dup****************/
						IF(ISNULL(@PrjPostFix, '') <> '')
						BEGIN
							IF EXISTS (
							SELECT * FROM dbo.CrmsysRollPermission
							WHERE FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmDefineProjects' )
							AND FormActionId = (SELECT Id FROM dbo.CrmsysFormActions WHERE ActionName = N'actNotCanSavePrjPostFixDup'
												AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmDefineProjects' ))
							AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
							BEGIN
								EXEC [MobspCheckDuplicatePrjPostFix]
									@PrjPostFix = @PrjPostFix,
									@CurrId = 0,
									@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
									@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID

								IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
								BEGIN
									SET @IsDuplicate = 1
								END
							END
						END
						/**************InquiryNum Check Dup****************/
						IF(ISNULL(@prjInquiryNum, '') <> '')
						BEGIN
							IF EXISTS (
							SELECT * FROM dbo.CrmsysRollPermission
							WHERE FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmDefineProjects' )
							AND FormActionId = (SELECT Id FROM dbo.CrmsysFormActions WHERE ActionName = N'actNotDupInquiryNumByCmpPrj'
												AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmDefineProjects' ))
							AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
							BEGIN
								EXEC [MobspCheckDuplicatePrjInquiryNum]
									@CompanyId = @InsertCmpId,
									@prjInquiryNum = @prjInquiryNum,
									@CurrId = 0,
									@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
									@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID

								IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
								BEGIN
									SET @IsDuplicate = 1
								END
							END
							ELSE IF EXISTS (
							SELECT * FROM dbo.CrmsysRollPermission
							WHERE FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmDefineProjects' )
							AND FormActionId = (SELECT Id FROM dbo.CrmsysFormActions WHERE ActionName = N'actNotDupInquiryNumByAllPrj'
												AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmDefineProjects' ))
							AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
							BEGIN
								EXEC [MobspCheckDuplicatePrjInquiryNum]
									@CompanyId = 0,
									@prjInquiryNum = @prjInquiryNum,
									@CurrId = 0,
									@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
									@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID

								IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
								BEGIN
									SET @IsDuplicate = 1
								END
							END
						END
						/**************NumR Check Dup****************/
						IF(ISNULL(@prjNumR, 0) <> 0)
						BEGIN
							DECLARE @CantSaveDupPrjNumRByCmpPrj BIT
							DECLARE @CantSaveDupPrjNumRByAllPrj BIT

							SELECT TOP 1 
								@CantSaveDupPrjNumRByCmpPrj = [IsActivePrjNumRByCmpPrj]
								,@CantSaveDupPrjNumRByAllPrj = [IsActivePrjNumRByAllPrj]
							FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting]


							IF(@CantSaveDupPrjNumRByCmpPrj = 1)
							BEGIN
								EXEC [MobspCheckDuplicatePrjNumR]
									@CompanyId = @InsertCmpId,
									@prjNumR = @prjNumR,
									@CurrId = 0,
									@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
									@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID

								IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
								BEGIN
									SET @IsDuplicate = 1
								END
							END
							ELSE IF(@CantSaveDupPrjNumRByAllPrj = 1)
							BEGIN
								EXEC [MobspCheckDuplicatePrjNumR]
									@CompanyId = 0,
									@prjNumR = @prjNumR,
									@CurrId = 0,
									@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
									@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID

								IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
								BEGIN
									SET @IsDuplicate = 1
								END
							END
						END
						/*AI14000201 - End*/
						/******************************/

						/*AI14021005 - Add OrderView to project*/
						IF(@prjOrderView IS NULL)
						BEGIN
							EXEC [dbo].[MobspReturnCompanyProjectOrderView]
							@CompanyId  = @InsertCmpId,
							@prjOrderView = @prjOrderView  OUTPUT
						END
						/*AI14021005 - End*/


						INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmProjects]
							   ([prjCompanyId]
							   ,[prjTitle]
							   ,[prjDesc]
							   ,[prjStartDate]
							   ,[prjStartDay]
							   ,[prjStartTime]
							   ,[prjStartDateTime]
							   ,[prjStarterUserId]
							   ,[prjFinishedStatusId]
							   ,[prjIsFinished]
							   ,[prjFinishedDate]
							   ,[prjFinishedDay]
							   ,[prjFinishedTime]
							   ,[prjFinishedDateTime]
							   ,[prjFinishedUserId]
							   ,[prjGradeId]
							   ,[prjPriorityId]
							   ,[prjFirstRegisterDate]
							   ,[prjFirstRegisterUserId]
							   ,[UserId]
							   ,[Year1]
							   ,[Month1]
							   ,[RegisterDate]
							   ,[RegisterDatePersian]
							   ,[prjGroupId]
							   ,[prjcnvsStatusGroupId]
							   ,[prjOrderView]
							   ,[AppType]
							   ,[prjPostFix]
							   ,[LanguageID]
							   ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
							   ,[FirstRegisterDateTime]
							   ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
							   ,[prjFirstRegisterDateTime]
							   ,[FirstRegisterDate]
							   ,[prjRemindDate]
							   ,[prjRemindDateTime]
							   /*RZ971103- Add field*/ 	
							   ,[prjFOCmpCanSee] 
							   ,[prjIsTicket] 
							   ,[prjUserDepartmentId]
								/*RZ971103- END*/
								/*AI14000131 - Add*/
								,[prjHaveSms]
								,[prjSmsPrsIds]
								,[prjSmsText]
								,[prjSmsSendDate] 
								,[prjSmsSendDatePersian]
								,[prjInquiryNum]
								,[prjSubject] 
								,[prjTitleGroup] 
								,[prjCategoryId] 
								,[prjDescI] 
								,[prjNumRef] 
								,[prjNumR]
								/*AI14000131 End*/
								/*AI14010317*/
								/*,[prjToDoTime] AI14010412*/
								,[prjToDoTimeRegister]
								,[prjToDoTimeDone]
								,[prjFOCmpCanInsCnvs]
								,[prjFOCmpCanNotInsCnvs]
								,[prjTicketStop]
								,[prjIsTicketNew]
								/*AI14010317-End*/
								,[prjToDoTimeSec] /*AI14010412*/
								,[prjToDoTimeRegisterHistory] /*AI14010416*/
								/*AI14010728*/
								,[prjcmpPresentationId]
								,[prjcmpCampaignIds]
								/*AI14010728-End*/
								,[prjcmpQuickRegisterDefSettingId] /*AI14020504*/
							)
						 VALUES
								(@InsertCmpId
							   ,@prjTitle
							   ,@prjDesc
							   ,@prjStartDate
							   ,@prjStartDay
							   ,@prjStartTime
							   ,@prjStartDateTime
							   ,@prjStarterUserId
							   ,@prjFinishedStatusId
							   ,@prjIsFinished
							   ,@prjFinishedDate
							   ,@prjFinishedDay
							   ,@prjFinishedTime
							   ,@prjFinishedDateTime
							   ,@prjFinishedUserId
							   ,@prjGradeId
							   ,@prjPriorityId
							   ,@RegisterDatePersian /*AI14010621 - @prjFirstRegisterDatePersian*/
							   ,@prjFirstRegisterUserId
							   ,@UserId
							   ,@Year1
							   ,@Month1
							   ,@RegisterDate
							   ,@RegisterDatePersian
							   ,@prjGroupId
							   ,@prjcnvsStatusGroupId
							   ,@prjOrderView
							   ,@AppType
							   ,@prjPostFix
							   ,@DefaultLanguageID
							   ,@prjFirstRegisterUserId
							   ,@RegisterDate  /*AI14010621 - @prjFirstRegisterDate*/
							   ,@RegisterDatePersian  /*AI14010621 - @prjFirstRegisterDatePersian*/
							   ,@RegisterDate  /*AI14010621 - @prjFirstRegisterDate*/
							   ,@RegisterDate  /*AI14010621 - @prjFirstRegisterDate*/
							   ,@prjRemindDate
							   ,@prjRemindDateTime
							   /*RZ971103- Add field*/ 	
							   ,@prjFOCmpCanSee 
							   ,@prjIsTicket 
							   ,@prjUserDepartmentId
								/*RZ971103- END*/
								/*AI14000131 - Added*/
								,@prjHaveSms
								,@prjSmsPrsIds
								,@prjSmsText 
								,@prjSmsSendDate 
								,@prjSmsSendDatePersian 
								,@prjInquiryNum
								,@prjSubject
								,@prjTitleGroup
								,@prjCategoryId
								,@prjDescI
								,@prjNumRef
								,@prjNumR
								/*AI14000131 End*/ 
								/*AI14010317*/
								/*,@prjToDoDateTime AI14010412*/
								,@prjToDoRegisterDateTime
								,@prjToDoDoneDateTime
								,@prjFOCmpCanInsCnvs
								,@prjFOCmpCanNotInsCnvs
								,@prjTicketStop
								,@prjIsTicketNew
								/*AI14010317-End*/
								,@prjToDoTimeSec /*AI14010412*/
								,@prjToDoTimeRegisterHistory /*AI14010416*/
								/*AI14010728*/
								,@prjcmpPresentationId
								,@prjcmpCampaignIds
								/*AI14010728-End*/
								,@prjcmpQuickRegisterDefSettingId /*AI14020504*/
								)           								          													
						SET @InsertPrjId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
						/******* Insert to lang table ********/
						INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmProjects_Lang]
						   ([ProjectID]
						   ,[LanguageID]
						   ,[prjTitle]
						   ,[prjDesc]
						   ,[prjPostFix])
						SELECT
							@InsertPrjId
							,LN.LanguageID
							,@prjTitle
							,@prjDesc
							,@prjPostFix
						FROM dbo.Languages LN 
						WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID	
						/*RZ961028- check deactive lang*/
						AND LN.IsActive =1
						/***** Insert project informations defualt field *****/						
						EXEC dbo.MobspInsertProjectInformationsDefualtField 
							@ProjectID = @InsertPrjId,
							@UserId = @UserId,
							@Year1 = @Year1,
							@Month1 = @Month1,
							@RegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
							@RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
							@AppType = @AppType,
							@LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID,
							@FirstRegisterUserId  = @prjFirstRegisterUserId,
							@FirstRegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian, /*AI14010621 - @prjFirstRegisterDatePersian*/
							@FirstRegisterDate = @RegisterDate /*AI14010621 - @prjFirstRegisterDate*/
						/*********** Insert to Forbidden *********************/
						IF(@prjInsertForbidden = 1)
						BEGIN
							EXEC dbo.MobspInsertCrmProjectsForbiddenForUserFromDefineProject 
							@CrmUserId = @UserId,
							@ForbiddenProjectTitle = @prjTitle,
							@FirstRegisterDate = @prjFirstRegisterDate,
							@FirstRegisterDatePersian = @prjFirstRegisterDatePersian,
							@FirstRegisterUserId  = @prjFirstRegisterUserId,
							@UserId  = @UserId,
							@Year1  = @CurrYear,
							@Month1 = @Month1,
							@RegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
							@RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
							@AppType = @AppType,
							@LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID  
						END	
						
						/*AI14010407*/
						IF(ISNULL(@prjIsTicket, 0) = 1 AND ISNULL(@prjIsFinished, 0) = 1)
						BEGIN
							EXEC [dbo].[MobspCloseTicket]
								@ProjectId = @InsertPrjId,
								@UserId  = @UserId,
								@Year1  = @CurrYear,
								@Month1 = @Month1,
								@RegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
								@RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
								@AppType = @AppType
						END
						/*AI14010407-End*/

						/*************** Ids inserted ********/				
						/*SET @InsertedProjectIds = @InsertedProjectIds + CAST(@InsertPrjId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '|'*/
						SET @InsertedPrjIdsInMobAndDb = @InsertedPrjIdsInMobAndDb + CAST(@ProjectID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ',' + CAST(@InsertPrjId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '|'
						SET @InsertedProjectIds = @InsertedPrjIdsInMobAndDb
					END
					ELSE /*when companyid <> @prjCompanyId that come from mobile*/
						SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '<PRJID>' + CAST(@ProjectID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '<\PRJID>'+ 'Msg=1'		    				 					
				/**/	
				FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrPrj INTO @ProjectID, @prjCompanyId, @prjTitle, @prjDesc, @prjStartDate, @prjStartDay, @prjStartTime,
						@prjStartDateTime, @prjStarterUserId, @prjFinishedStatusId, @prjIsFinished, @prjFinishedDate,
						@prjFinishedDay, @prjFinishedTime, @prjFinishedDateTime, @prjFinishedUserId, @prjGradeId,
						@prjPriorityId, @prjFirstRegisterUserId, @prjGroupId, @prjcnvsStatusGroupId, @prjOrderView, @prjPostFix,
						@prjFirstRegisterDatePersian, @prjFirstRegisterDate, @prjRemindDate, @prjRemindDateTime, @prjInsertForbidden,
						/*RZ971103- Add field*/ 	/*RZ971103- END*/ /*AI14000131*/
						@prjFOCmpCanSee, @prjIsTicket, @prjUserDepartmentId,@prjHaveSms,@prjSmsPrsIds,@prjSmsText ,@prjSmsSendDate ,
						@prjSmsSendDatePersian ,@prjInquiryNum,@prjSubject,@prjTitleGroup,@prjCategoryId,@prjDescI,@prjNumRef,@prjNumR
						/*AI14010317*/,@prjToDoDateTime, @prjToDoRegisterDateTime, @prjToDoDoneDateTime, @prjFOCmpCanInsCnvs, @prjFOCmpCanNotInsCnvs, @prjTicketStop, @prjIsTicketNew  /*AI14010317*/
						,@prjToDoTimeSec /*AI14010412*/, @prjToDoTimeRegisterHistory /*AI14010416*/, @prjcmpPresentationId, @prjcmpCampaignIds, /*AI14010728*/
						@prjcmpQuickRegisterDefSettingId /*AI14020504*/
				END
				CLOSE CrsrPrj
				DEALLOCATE CrsrPrj   					 	 	 
		END
	END
END
ELSE /*Not insert company*/
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=0' 	
/**/
IF (@@ERROR > 0)
BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert Project' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
ELSE 
COMMIT TRAN

														@CnvsCoordinateLongitude = @cnvsCoordinateLongitude,
														@CnvsCoordinateLastDate = @cnvsCoordinateLastDate,
														@CnvsCoordinateLastDatePersian = @cnvsCoordinateLastDatePersian,
														@EventPolicyNeedCheck = @cnvsEventPolicyNeedCheck,
														/*RZ971103- Add field*/ 	
														@CnvsFOCmpCanSee = @cnvsFOCmpCanSee,
														@CnvsIsTicket = @cnvsIsTicket,	
														@CnvsTicketIsRead = @cnvsTicketIsRead,
														@CnvsDoingTime = @cnvsDoingTime,
														@CnvsIsCopy = @cnvsIsCopy,
														/*RZ971103- END*/		
														/*AI14010414*/
														@CnvsDoingTimeSec = @cnvsDoingTimeSec,
														@CnvsStatusProcessKindId = @cnvsStatusProcessKindId,
														@CnvsStatusGroupId = @cnvsStatusGroupId,
														@IsCloseTicket = NULL,
														/*AI14010414*/
														/*AI14010602*/
														@AvoidSendSms = @cnvsAvoidSendSms,
														@AvoidSendEmail = @cnvsAvoidSendEmail,
														@EmailEventPolicyNeedCheck = @cnvsEmailEventPolicyNeedCheck,
														/*AI14010602-End*/
														@CnvsHappyVote = @cnvsHappyVote, /*AI14010609*/
														@CnvsStatusResultId = @cnvsStatusResultId, /*AI14010908*/
														/*AI14010909*/
														@SurveyMainInternalLink = @SurveyMainInternalLink,
														@SurveyMainExtrenalLink = @SurveyMainExtrenalLink,
														@SurveyMainExtrenalLinkLogin = @SurveyMainExtrenalLinkLogin,
														@EventConversationErjaShowText = @EventConversationErjaShowText,
														@EventErjaRefmsg = @EventErjaRefmsg,
														/*AI14010909-End*/
														@CnvsKnowledgeTankIds = @cnvsKnowledgeTankIds, /*AI14020310*/
														/*AI14020429*/
														@CnvsDoingTimeFrom = @cnvsDoingTimeFrom,
														@CnvsDoingTimeTo = @cnvsDoingTimeTo,
														/*AI14020429-End*/
														@CnvsIdInserted = @InsertCnvsId OUTPUT	,
														@ResultRunCnvsEventPolicyForSms = @ResultRunCnvsEventPolicyForSms OUTPUT,
														/*GHO14030129 - Add can set cnvsRemindDate and cnvsRemindDateForAllarm to isCopy*/
														@cnvsIsCopyRemindDate = @cnvsIsCopyRemindDate,
														@cnvsIsCopyRemindDateForAllarm =@cnvsIsCopyRemindDateForAllarm
														/*GHO14030129-End*/
															
														IF(@ResultRunCnvsEventPolicyForSms = -1)
															SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '<CNVSID>' + CAST(@ConversationID AS NVARCHAR(10))+ '<\CNVSID>' + 'Msg=6'	/*not insert sms */					
																														 																																										     								     				
														/*************** Ids inserted ********/				
														SET @InsertedConversationIds = @InsertedConversationIds + CAST(@ConversationID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ',' + CAST(@InsertCnvsId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '|'   /*cnvsIdMob,cnvsIdDb*/
														/**/
														/*RZ970502- Add check ForcedStatus*/
														END
														ELSE
															SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '<CNVS><CNVSID>' + CAST(@ConversationID AS NVARCHAR(10))+ '<\CNVSID>' + '<ForcedStatus>' + @ConversationForcedStatus +'<\ForcedStatus><\CNVS>'	/*ForcedStatus */					
															
														/*RZ970502- Add check ForcedStatus END*/
														
													END
													ELSE
														SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '<CNVSID>' + CAST(@ConversationID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '<\CNVSID>' + '<PRJID>' + CAST(@cnvsProjectId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '<\PRJID>'+ 'Msg=5'	/*not insert cnvsProject */					
													
												END
												ELSE
													SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '<CNVSID>' + CAST(@ConversationID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '<\CNVSID>' + '<PRJID>' + CAST(@cnvsProjectId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '<\PRJID>' + 'Msg=5'	/*not insert cnvsProject */																																		
											END																																
										/*******************************************************/
									END
									ELSE
									 SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '<CNVSID>' + CAST(@ConversationID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '<\CNVSID>' + '<PRSID>' + CAST(@cnvsPersonId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '<\PRSID>' + 'Msg=3'	/*not insert cnvsperson */					
								END
								ELSE
									SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '<CNVSID>' + CAST(@ConversationID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '<\CNVSID>' + '<PRSID>' + CAST(@cnvsPersonId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '<\PRSID>' + 'Msg=3'	/*not insert cnvsperson */
							
																			
							END
							/**/												       																										
						END
						ELSE /*when companyid <> @cnvsCompanyId that come from mobile*/
							SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '<CNVSID>' + CAST(@ConversationID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '<\CNVSID>' + 'Msg=1'		    				 					
					/**/	
					FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrCnvs INTO @ConversationID, @cnvsProjectId, @cnvsCompanyId, @cnvsDate, @cnvsDay, @cnvsTime, @cnvsDateTime,
							@cnvsTopicId, @cnvsSubject, @cnvsDesc, @cnvsPersonId, @cnvsUserId, @cnvsReferUserId, @cnvsReferDesc,
							@cnvsStatusId, @cnvsPriorityId, @cnvsRemindDate, @cnvsRemindTime, @cnvsRemindDateTime, @cnvsRemindOk,
							@cnvsIsBookMark, @cnvsFirstRegisterUserId, @cnvsFirstRegisterDatePersian, @cnvsFirstRegisterDate,		
							@cnvsRemindDateForAllarm, @cnvsRemindDateTimeForAllarm, @ApiId, @cnvsCoordinateLatitude,
							@cnvsCoordinateLongitude, @cnvsCoordinateLastDate, @cnvsCoordinateLastDatePersian, @cnvsEventPolicyNeedCheck, @TmpUserMultiUserText,
							/*RZ971103- Add field*/ 	
							@cnvsFOCmpCanSee, @cnvsIsTicket, @cnvsTicketIsRead, @cnvsDoingTime, @cnvsIsCopy
							/*RZ971103- END*/
							/*AI14010414*/
							,@cnvsDoingTimeSec, @cnvsStatusProcessKindId, @cnvsStatusGroupId
							/*AI14010414-End*/
							,@cnvsEmailEventPolicyNeedCheck, @cnvsAvoidSendSms, @cnvsAvoidSendEmail /*AI14010602*/ ,@cnvsHappyVote /*AI14010609*/
							,@cnvsStatusResultId /*AI14010908*/ ,@cnvsKnowledgeTankIds /*AI14020310*/,@cnvsDoingTimeFrom , @cnvsDoingTimeTo/*AI14020429*/
							,@cnvsIsCopyRemindDate /*GHO14030129*/,@cnvsIsCopyRemindDateForAllarm /*GHO14030129*/
					END
					CLOSE CrsrCnvs
					DEALLOCATE CrsrCnvs					 	 	 
				END
			/*******************************************************/																
		END
		ELSE/*Not insert projects*/	
			SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=4' 																								
		/*******************************************************/										
	END
	ELSE /*Not insert persons*/
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=2'	
	/*******************************************************/
	END
END
ELSE /*Not insert company*/
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=0' 
/**/
IF (@@ERROR > 0)
BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert Conversation' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
ELSE 
COMMIT TRAN
/**/


/********************************************************************************/
/*************************** Insert ComapnyAddress ******************************/
/********************************************************************************/
/*AI14021009*/
BEGIN TRAN
/**/
/*check company is inserted*/
IF(@InsertedCompanyId > 0)
BEGIN
	IF (@CompanyAddressList IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
		IF(@IsDuplicate = 0) /* @IsDuplicate = 0 ====> insert to main table*/
		BEGIN
			DECLARE CrsrCmpAdd Cursor For
					SELECT 
						[CompanyAddressId], [cmpaddCompanyId], [cmpaddCityId], [CompanyAddress], [cmpaddDesc], [cmpaddZipCode], [cmpaddPostBox],
						[cmpaddCoordinateLatitude], [cmpaddCoordinateLongitude], [cmpaddCoordinateLastDate], [cmpaddCoordinateLastDatePersian],
						[cmpaddCoordinateZoomLevel], [cmpaddCoordinateLastUserId], [cmpaddTypeId]

					FROM @CompanyAddressTmpTable		
				/**/
				OPEN CrsrCmpAdd
				FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrCmpAdd INTO @CompanyAddressId, @cmpaddCompanyId, @cmpaddCityId, @CompanyAddress, @cmpaddDesc, @cmpaddZipCode, @cmpaddPostBox,
						@cmpaddCoordinateLatitude, @cmpaddCoordinateLongitude, @cmpaddCoordinateLastDate, @cmpaddCoordinateLastDatePersian,
						@cmpaddCoordinateZoomLevel, @cmpaddCoordinateLastUserId, @cmpaddTypeId
				WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
				BEGIN
				/**/
					IF(@cmpaddCompanyId = @CompanyID)
					BEGIN
						INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompanyAddresses]
							   ([cmpaddCompanyId]
							   ,[cmpaddCityId]
							   ,[CompanyAddress]
							   ,[cmpaddDesc]
							   ,[cmpaddZipCode]
							   ,[cmpaddPostBox]
							   ,[cmpaddCoordinateLatitude]
							   ,[cmpaddCoordinateLongitude]
							   ,[cmpaddCoordinateLastDate]
							   ,[cmpaddCoordinateLastDatePersian]
							   ,[cmpaddCoordinateZoomLevel]
							   ,[cmpaddCoordinateLastUserId]
							   ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
							   ,[FirstRegisterDate]
							   ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
							   ,[UserId]
							   ,[Year1]
							   ,[Month1]
							   ,[RegisterDate]
							   ,[RegisterDatePersian]
							   ,[AppType]
							   ,[LanguageID]
							   ,[cmpaddTypeId])
						 VALUES
							   (@InsertCmpId
							   ,@cmpaddCityId
							   ,@CompanyAddress
							   ,@cmpaddDesc
							   ,@cmpaddZipCode
							   ,@cmpaddPostBox
							   ,@cmpaddCoordinateLatitude
							   ,@cmpaddCoordinateLongitude
							   ,@cmpaddCoordinateLastDate
							   ,@cmpaddCoordinateLastDatePersian
							   ,@cmpaddCoordinateZoomLevel
							   ,@cmpaddCoordinateLastUserId
							   ,@UserId
							   ,@RegisterDate
							   ,@RegisterDatePersian
							   ,@UserId
							   ,@Year1
							   ,@Month1
							   ,@RegisterDate
							   ,@RegisterDatePersian
							   ,@AppType
							   ,@LanguageID
							   ,@cmpaddTypeId
							   )
						SET @InsertedCmpAddressId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
						/******* Insert to lang table ********/
						INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompanyAddresses_Lang]
							(
							[CompanyAddressId]
							,[LanguageID]
							,[CompanyAddress]
							,[cmpaddDesc]
							)
						SELECT
							@InsertedCmpAddressId
							,LN.LanguageID
							,@CompanyAddress
							,@cmpaddDesc
						FROM dbo.Languages LN 
						WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID	
						AND ISNULL(LN.IsActive,0) = 1
						

						/*************** Ids inserted ********/				
						SET @InsertedCmpAddIdsInMobAndDb = @InsertedCmpAddIdsInMobAndDb + CAST(@CompanyAddressId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ',' + CAST(@InsertedCmpAddressId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '|'
						SET @InsertedCompanyAddressIds = @InsertedCmpAddIdsInMobAndDb
					END
					ELSE /*when companyid <> @cmpaddCompanyId that come from mobile*/
						SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '<CADID>' + CAST(@ProjectID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '<\CADID>'+ 'Msg=1'		    				 					
				/**/	
				FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrCmpAdd INTO @CompanyAddressId, @cmpaddCompanyId, @cmpaddCityId, @CompanyAddress, @cmpaddDesc, @cmpaddZipCode, @cmpaddPostBox,
						@cmpaddCoordinateLatitude, @cmpaddCoordinateLongitude, @cmpaddCoordinateLastDate, @cmpaddCoordinateLastDatePersian,
						@cmpaddCoordinateZoomLevel, @cmpaddCoordinateLastUserId, @cmpaddTypeId
				END
				CLOSE CrsrCmpAdd
				DEALLOCATE CrsrCmpAdd   					 	 	 
		END
	END
END
ELSE /*Not insert company*/
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=0' 	
/**/
IF (@@ERROR > 0)
BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert CompanyAddress' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
ELSE 
COMMIT TRAN
/*AI14021009-End*/

/*RZ961004- add TypeTable*/
IF(@IsDuplicate = 1) /* @IsDuplicate = 1 ====> Duplicate*/
BEGIN
	SET @TypeTable = N'Dup'
END
ELSE
	SET @TypeTable = N''
/**/
/*RZ970508- Add rollback when have error Start*/
IF(@ErrorMessage IS NOT NULL AND @ErrorMessage <> '')
BEGIN
	ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
 	SELECT 1
	SET @InsertedCompanyId = @CompanyIdInError
	SET @InsertedPersonIds = @PersonIdsInError
	SET @InsertedProjectIds = @ProjectIdsInError
	SET @InsertedConversationIds = @ConversationIdsInError
	SET @InsertedCompanyAddressIds = @CompanyAddressIdsInError /*AI14021009*/
END
/*RZ970508- Add rollback when have error End*/
/**/
IF (@ErrorNumber > 0)
BEGIN 
	RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)
	ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main

 	SELECT 0
END 
ELSE
BEGIN

	/*AI140010128*//*AI14010122*/
	EXEC [dbo].[MobspCreateCompanyUsernameAndPassword] @InsertedCompanyId, @UserId, @Month1, @Year1, @RegisterDate, @RegisterDatePersian, @AppType
	
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
	/*SET @InsertedCompanyId = @InsertedCmpId 
	SET @InsertedPersonId = @InsertedPrsId 
	SET @InsertedProjectId = @InsertedPrjId
	SET @InsertedConversationId = @InsertedCNVSId*/ 	
 	SELECT 1
END	
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[MobspInsertQuickCompany] | References permission/temp-filter machinery





/*RZ961002*/
/*RZ961004-change |#| to |#|*/
/*RZ961017- convert image*/
/*RZ961027- change search cmpname*/
/*RZ961028- check deactive lang*/
/*RZ961129- Multi language*/
/*RZ961206- Add time to date*/
/*RZ971219- Remove check dublicate in tel and other fields*/
/*RZ971222- Add check access to data permission*/
/*RZ990509- Add cmpKind*/
/*AI14011027 - Convert Arabic characters to Persian*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspInsertQuickCompany]
/*
	EXEC MobspInsertQuickCompany  	
*/
(
@Company NVARCHAR(MAX) ,
@UserId INT,
@Year1 SMALLINT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@AppType VARCHAR(20), 
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL,
@PersonLabel NVARCHAR(50),
/**/
@InsertedCompanyId INT OUTPUT,
@ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** TRANSACTION *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/***************************** Define fields ************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
DECLARE @IsDuplicate BIT,
/*********** Company ***********/
@CompanyId INT,
@cmpImportDate NVARCHAR(8),
@cmpImportDateTime DATETIME,
/*@cmpNumber INT,*/
@cmpName NVARCHAR(300),
@cmpNameEng NVARCHAR(300),
@cmpTitleId INT,
@cmpFame NVARCHAR(200),
@cmpActivityType NVARCHAR(100),
@cmpGroupId INT,
@cmpSubGroupId INT,
@cmpGroupIdII INT,
@cmpGroupIdIII INT,
@cmpGroupIdIV INT,
@cmpTypeId INT,
@cmpPresentationId INT,
@cmpPreTel NVARCHAR(10),
@cmpTel NVARCHAR(15),
@cmpTelUpTo NVARCHAR(10),
@cmpTelDesc NVARCHAR(50),
@cmpPreTelII NVARCHAR(10),
@cmpTelII NVARCHAR(15),
@cmpTelUpToII NVARCHAR(10),
@cmpTelIIDesc NVARCHAR(50),
@cmpMobile NVARCHAR(15),
@cmpFax NVARCHAR(15),
@cmpFaxDesc NVARCHAR(50),
@cmpCountryId INT,
@cmpProvinceId INT,
@cmpCityId INT,
@cmpAddress NVARCHAR(250),
@cmpAddressDesc NVARCHAR(100),
@cmpZipCode NVARCHAR(50),
@cmpPostBox NVARCHAR(50),
@cmpWebSite NVARCHAR(50),
@cmpEmail NVARCHAR(50),
@cmpInteresting NVARCHAR(500),
@cmpSpecialPoint NVARCHAR(500),
@cmpDescription NVARCHAR(500),
@cmpUserName NVARCHAR(50),
@cmpPassword NVARCHAR(50),
@cmpRegistrationNumber NVARCHAR(50),
@cmpEconomicCode NVARCHAR(50),
@cmpNationalCode NVARCHAR(50),
@cmpBuyLimit NVARCHAR(100),
@cmpAccountNumber NVARCHAR(500),
@cmpAccInquiry NVARCHAR(500),
@cmpFreight NVARCHAR(200),
@cmpInquiry NVARCHAR(500),
@cmpReferUserId INT,
@cmpSpecialWordForCI VARCHAR(300),
@cmpCode NVARCHAR(500),
@cmpEffectiveDatePersian VARCHAR(8),
@cmpEffectiveDateTime DATETIME,
@cmpFirstRegisterUserId INT,
@cmpFirstRegisterDatePersian VARCHAR(8),
@cmpFirstRegisterDate DATETIME,
@cmpCoordinateLatitude NVARCHAR(100),
@cmpCoordinateLongitude NVARCHAR(100),
@cmpCoordinateLastDate DATETIME,
@cmpCoordinateLastDatePersian VARCHAR(8),
@cmpCoordinateZoomLevel INT,
@cmpCoordinateLastUserId INT,
@cmpMultiGroups NVARCHAR(500),
@CompanyLogo VARBINARY(MAX)/*IMAGE*/
/************************************ Current Year ******************************/
DECLARE @CurrYear SMALLINT
SET @CurrYear = CAST(SUBSTRING(@RegisterDatePersian, 1, 4) AS SMALLINT)	
/*RZ961206- Add time to date*/
DECLARE @RegisterTime NVARCHAR(8)
SET @RegisterTime = (CONVERT(CHAR(8), @RegisterDate, 108))					
/************************************ Error *************************************/
DECLARE @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50), @ErrorNumber INT
/********************************************************************************/
/***************************** Split fields *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
SET @IsDuplicate  = 0
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** Company *****************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRAN
/**/
IF(@Company IS NOT NULL AND @Company <> '')
BEGIN
DECLARE @CmpField NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
WHILE LEN(@Company) > 0
BEGIN
    IF PATINDEX('%|#|%', @Company) > 0
    BEGIN
        SET @CmpField = SUBSTRING(@Company,
                                    0,
                                    PATINDEX('%|#|%', @Company))
        /*SELECT @CmpField*/
        /*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
        /*Get parameter name and split value form parameter*/
        /*For example => cmpTypeId=102 => cmpTypeId= => cmpTypeId => Compare => value*/
        DECLARE @CmpFieldValue NVARCHAR(MAX)
        DECLARE @CmpFieldName NVARCHAR(200)
        SET @CmpFieldName = SUBSTRING(@CmpField, 1, CHARINDEX('=',@CmpField)-1)                                    
		SET @CmpFieldValue = SUBSTRING(@CmpField, CHARINDEX('=', @CmpField)+1, Len(@CmpField))
        /*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
        /*---------------------------- BEGIN Fill variabele ------------------------------*/
        /*BEGIN COMPANY*/
        IF @CmpFieldName = 'CompanyID'
        BEGIN
			SELECT @CompanyId = CASE WHEN CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) = -1 THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) END
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpImportDate'
        BEGIN
			SET @cmpImportDate = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = '' THEN NULL ELSE REPLACE(@CmpFieldValue, '/', '') END
			/*RZ961206- Add time to date*/							
			SET @cmpImportDateTime = CASE WHEN @cmpImportDate IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@cmpImportDate, @RegisterTime) END			 	
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpName'
        BEGIN
			SET @cmpName = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = '' THEN NULL ELSE @CmpFieldValue END
			SET @cmpName = (SELECT dbo.[fnConvertArabicCharacterToPersian](@cmpName)) /*AI14011027*/
			/*IF(@cmpName IS NOT NULL AND @cmpName <> @PersonLabel)/*check duplicate*/
			BEGIN					
				DECLARE	@ResultCheckDuplicate INT = 0	
				EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicateCompany]
						@Type  = 0, 
						@Id = 0, 
						@Val = @cmpName, 
						@TitleID = 0, 
						@CityId = 0, 
						@UserID  = @UserId ,							
						@Result = @ResultCheckDuplicate OUTPUT,
						/*RZ961129- Multi language*/
						@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
						@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID
				IF(@ResultCheckDuplicate > 0)
					SET @IsDuplicate = 1						
			END	*/		
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpNameEng'
        BEGIN
			SET @cmpNameEng = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = '' THEN NULL ELSE @CmpFieldValue END
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpTitleId'
        BEGIN
			SELECT @cmpTitleId = CASE WHEN CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) = -1 THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) END
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpFame'
        BEGIN
			SET @cmpFame = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = '' THEN NULL ELSE @CmpFieldValue END
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpActivityType'
        BEGIN
			SET @cmpActivityType = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = '' THEN NULL ELSE @CmpFieldValue END
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpGroupId'
        BEGIN
			SELECT @cmpGroupId = CASE WHEN CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) = -1 THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) END

			/*RZ971222- Add check access to data permission*/
			IF(@cmpGroupId IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
				DECLARE @IsAccessGroupId BIT = 0

				EXEC dbo.MobspCheckCmpDataPermissionForUser 	
				@Mode = 3,
				@Id = @cmpGroupId,
				@CrmUserId = @UserId,
				@IsAccess = @IsAccessGroupId OUTPUT
				
				IF(@IsAccessGroupId = 0)
				BEGIN
					DECLARE @GroupName NVARCHAR(1000)
					SET @GroupName = (SELECT
										CASE  WHEN (@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
											THEN (SELECT GroupName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroups CG WHERE CG.CompanyGroupID = @cmpGroupId)
											ELSE (SELECT GroupName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroups_Lang CG WHERE CG.CompanyGroupID = @cmpGroupId AND CG.LanguageID = @LanguageID)
										END
									 )

					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=CmpGroupIdAccess|' + ISNULL(@GroupName,'') + '|Msg=CmpGroupIdAccess|'
				END	 
			END
			/*RZ971222- END*/
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpSubGroupId'
        BEGIN
			SELECT @cmpSubGroupId = CASE WHEN CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) = -1 THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) END

			/*RZ971222- Add check access to data permission*/
			IF(@cmpSubGroupId IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
				DECLARE @IsAccessSubGroupId BIT = 0

				EXEC dbo.MobspCheckCmpDataPermissionForUser 	
				@Mode = 4,
				@Id = @cmpSubGroupId,
				@CrmUserId = @UserId,
				@IsAccess = @IsAccessSubGroupId OUTPUT
				
				IF(@IsAccessSubGroupId = 0)
				BEGIN
					DECLARE @SubGroupName NVARCHAR(1000)
					SET @SubGroupName = (SELECT
										CASE  WHEN (@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
											THEN (SELECT SubGroupName FROM dbo.CrmCompanySubGroups CG WHERE CG.CompanySubGroupID = @cmpSubGroupId)
											ELSE (SELECT SubGroupName FROM dbo.CrmCompanySubGroups_Lang CG WHERE CG.CompanySubGroupID = @cmpSubGroupId AND CG.LanguageID = @LanguageID)
										END
									 )

					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=CmpSubGroupIdAccess|' + ISNULL(@SubGroupName,'') + '|Msg=CmpSubGroupIdAccess|'
					
				END																				
			END
			/*RZ971222- END*/
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpGroupIdII'
        BEGIN
			SELECT @cmpGroupIdII = CASE WHEN CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) = -1 THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) END		
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpGroupIdIII'
        BEGIN
			SELECT @cmpGroupIdIII = CASE WHEN CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) = -1 THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) END
        END
        /*--------------------------*/
		IF @CmpFieldName = 'cmpGroupIdIV'
        BEGIN
			SELECT @cmpGroupIdIV = CASE WHEN CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) = -1 THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS INT) END

			/*RZ971222- Add check access to data permission*/
			IF(@cmpGroupIdIV IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
				DECLARE @IsAccessGroupIdIVId BIT = 0

				EXEC dbo.MobspCheckCmpDataPermissionForUser 	
				@Mode = 5,
				@Id = @cmpGroupIdIV,
				@CrmUserId = @UserId,
				@IsAccess = @IsAccessGroupIdIVId OUTPUT
				
				IF(@IsAccessGroupIdIVId = 0)
				BEGIN
					DECLARE @GroupIVName NVARCHAR(1000)

					SET @GroupIVName = (SELECT
										CASE  WHEN (@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
											THEN (SELECT GroupIVName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroupsIV CG WHERE CG.CompanyGroupIVID = @cmpGroupIdIV)
											ELSE (SELECT GroupIVName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroupsIV_Lang CG WHERE CG.CompanyGroupIVID = @cmpGroupIdIV AND CG.LanguageID = @LanguageID)
										END
									 )
										
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=CmpGroupIdIVAccess|' + ISNULL(@GroupIVName,'') + '|Msg=CmpGroupIdIVAccess|'
				END
			END
			/*RZ971222- END*/
        END

				@IsAccess = @IsAccessProvinceId OUTPUT
				
				IF(@IsAccessProvinceId = 0)
				BEGIN
					DECLARE @ProvinceName NVARCHAR(1000)
					SET @ProvinceName = (SELECT
										CASE  WHEN (@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
											THEN (SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province P WHERE P.ProvinceID = @cmpProvinceId)
											ELSE (SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province_Lang P WHERE P.ProvinceID = @cmpProvinceId AND P.LanguageID = @LanguageID)
										END

				@Mode = 2,
				@Id = @cmpCityId,
				@CrmUserId = @UserId,
				@IsAccess = @IsAccessCityId OUTPUT

				BEGIN
					DECLARE @CityName NVARCHAR(1000)

										CASE  WHEN (@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
											THEN (SELECT City FROM dbo.City C WHERE C.CityID = @cmpCityId)
											ELSE (SELECT City FROM dbo.City_Lang C WHERE C.CityID = @cmpCityId AND C.LanguageID = @LanguageID)
										END

			/*RZ961206- Add time to date*/							
			SET @cmpEffectiveDateTime = CASE WHEN @cmpEffectiveDatePersian IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@cmpEffectiveDatePersian, @RegisterTime) END			
        END

        END
       /*--------------------------*/

        BEGIN

		IF @CmpFieldName = 'CompanyLogo'
        BEGIN
			/*SET @CompanyLogo = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = '' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CmpFieldValue AS VARBINARY(MAX)) END*/
			/*RZ961017- convert image*/
			SET @CompanyLogo = CASE WHEN @CmpFieldValue = '' THEN NULL ELSE 
				(SELECT CAST(N'' AS xml).value('xs:base64Binary(sql:variable("@CmpFieldValue"))', 'varbinary(max)'))			
			END
        END       
        /*END COMPANY*/		
        /*END GENERAL*/
        /*---------------------------- END Fill variabele --------------------------------*/
        /*---------------------------- Remove current parameter from listparam ------------*/
        SET @Company = SUBSTRING(@Company,
                                  LEN(@CmpField + '|#|') + 1,
                                  LEN(@Company))
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SET @CmpField = @Company
        SET @Company = NULL
        /*SELECT @CmpField*/
    END
END
/************************** Check duplicate before post *************************/
/*RZ961027- change search cmpname*/
IF(@cmpName IS NOT NULL AND @cmpName <> @PersonLabel)/*check duplicate*/
BEGIN
DECLARE @TitleId INT , @CityId INT
SELECT @TitleId = CASE WHEN @cmpTitleId IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE @cmpTitleId END
SELECT @CityId = CASE WHEN @cmpCityId IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE @cmpCityId END
					
	DECLARE	@ResultCheckDuplicateForCmpName INT = 0	
	EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicateCompany]
			@Type  = 1, 
			@Id = 0, 
			@Val = @cmpName, 
			@TitleID = @TitleId, 
			@CityId = @CityId, 
			@UserID  = @UserId ,							
			/*@Result = @ResultCheckDuplicateForCmpName OUTPUT, AI14010529 - Because sp changed*/
			/*RZ961129- Multi language*/
			@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
			@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID

	SET @ResultCheckDuplicateForCmpName = @@ROWCOUNT
	IF(@ResultCheckDuplicateForCmpName > 0)
		SET @IsDuplicate = 1						
END	

END
ELSE
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=0'

DECLARE @InsertCmpId INT
BEGIN TRAN
/**/
/** Get company number **/
DECLARE @cmpNumber INT 
SET @cmpNumber = (SELECT [dbo].[CrmfnReturnCompanyNumber]())

IF(@IsDuplicate = 1) /* @IsDuplicate = 1 ====> insert to tmp table*/
BEGIN
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'Dup=1Msg=0'				                           
END
ELSE IF(@ErrorMessage IS NULL OR  @ErrorMessage = '')/* @IsDuplicate = 0 ====> insert to main table*/
BEGIN
	/*==================================*/
	/*RZ990509- Add cmpKind*/
	DECLARE @cmpKind INT = 0
	IF((REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@cmpName AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) 
	 = REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(N'شخص' AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) ))
	 BEGIN
		SET @cmpKind = 1
	 END
	/*RZ990509- End*/
	/*==================================*/

	INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompany]
           (
            [ImportDate]
           ,[ImportDateTime]
           ,[cmpNumber]
           ,[cmpName]
           ,[cmpNameEng]
           ,[cmpTitleId]
           ,[cmpFame]
           ,[cmpActivityType]
           ,[cmpGroupId]
           ,[cmpSubGroupId]
           ,[cmpGroupIdII]
           ,[cmpGroupIdIII]
           ,[cmpGroupIdIV]
           ,[cmpTypeId]
           ,[cmpPresentationId]
           ,[cmpPreTel]
           ,[cmpTel]
           ,[cmpTelUpTo]
           ,[cmpTelDesc]
           ,[cmpPreTelII]
           ,[cmpTelII]
           ,[cmpTelUpToII]
           ,[cmpTelIIDesc]
           ,[cmpMobile]
           ,[cmpFax]
           ,[cmpFaxDesc]
           ,[cmpCountryId]
           ,[cmpProvinceId]
           ,[cmpCityId]
           ,[cmpAddress]
           ,[cmpAddressDesc]
           ,[cmpZipCode]
           ,[cmpPostBox]
           ,[cmpWebSite]
           ,[cmpEmail]
           ,[cmpInteresting]
           ,[cmpSpecialPoint]
           ,[cmpDescription]
           ,[cmpUserName]
           ,[cmpPassword]
           ,[cmpFirstRegisterUserId]
           ,[UserId]
           ,[Year1]
           ,[Month1]
           ,[RegisterDate]
           ,[RegisterDatePersian]
           ,[cmpRegistrationNumber]
           ,[cmpEconomicCode]
           ,[cmpNationalCode]
           ,[cmpBuyLimit]
           ,[cmpAccountNumber]
           ,[cmpAccInquiry]
           ,[cmpFreight]
           ,[cmpInquiry]
           ,[cmpReferUserId]
           ,[AppType]
           ,[cmpSpecialWordForCI]
           ,[cmpCode]
           ,[cmpEffectiveDatePersian]
           ,[cmpEffectiveDateTime]
           ,[LanguageID]
           ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
           ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
           ,[FirstRegisterDate]
           ,[cmpCoordinateLatitude]
           ,[cmpCoordinateLongitude]
           ,[cmpCoordinateLastDate]
           ,[cmpCoordinateLastDatePersian]
           ,[cmpCoordinateZoomLevel]
           ,[cmpCoordinateLastUserId]
		   ,[cmpKind]/*RZ990509- Add cmpKind*/
           )
     VALUES
           (
            @cmpImportDate
           ,@cmpImportDateTime
           ,@cmpNumber
           ,@cmpName
           ,@cmpNameEng
           ,@cmpTitleId
           ,@cmpFame
           ,@cmpActivityType
           ,@cmpGroupId
           ,@cmpSubGroupId
           ,@cmpGroupIdII
           ,@cmpGroupIdIII
           ,@cmpGroupIdIV
           ,@cmpTypeId
           ,@cmpPresentationId
           ,@cmpPreTel
           ,@cmpTel
           ,@cmpTelUpTo
           ,@cmpTelDesc

           ,@cmpTelII

           ,@cmpFaxDesc

           ,@cmpAddress
           ,@cmpAddressDesc
           ,@cmpZipCode
           ,@cmpPostBox
           ,@cmpWebSite
           ,@cmpEmail
           ,@cmpInteresting
           ,@cmpSpecialPoint
           ,@cmpDescription
           ,@cmpUserName
           ,@cmpPassword
           ,@cmpFirstRegisterUserId
           ,@UserId
           ,@Year1
           ,@Month1
           ,@RegisterDate
           ,@RegisterDatePersian
           ,@cmpRegistrationNumber
           ,@cmpEconomicCode
           ,@cmpNationalCode
           ,@cmpBuyLimit
           ,@cmpAccountNumber
           ,@cmpAccInquiry
           ,@cmpFreight
           ,@cmpInquiry
           ,@cmpReferUserId
           ,@AppType
           ,@cmpSpecialWordForCI
           ,@cmpCode
           ,@cmpEffectiveDatePersian
           ,@cmpEffectiveDateTime
           ,@DefaultLanguageID
           ,@cmpFirstRegisterUserId
           ,@cmpFirstRegisterDatePersian
           ,@cmpFirstRegisterDate

           ,@cmpCoordinateLongitude
           ,@cmpCoordinateLastDate
           ,@cmpCoordinateLastDatePersian
           ,@cmpCoordinateZoomLevel
           ,@cmpCoordinateLastUserId
		   ,@cmpKind/*RZ990509- Add cmpKind*/
           )
    SET @InsertCmpId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
	/*********** Insert to lang table ***********/
	INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang]
           (
			[CompanyID]
           ,[LanguageID]
           ,[cmpName]
           ,[cmpFame]
           ,[cmpActivityType]
           ,[cmpTelDesc]
           ,[cmpTelIIDesc]
           ,[cmpFaxDesc]
           ,[cmpAddress]
           ,[cmpAddressDesc]
           ,[cmpInteresting]
           ,[cmpSpecialPoint]
           ,[cmpDescription]
           ,[cmpSpecialWordForCI]
           ,[cmpInquiry]
           )
     SELECT
			@InsertCmpId
		   ,LN.LanguageID
		   ,@cmpName
           ,@cmpFame
           ,@cmpActivityType
           ,@cmpTelDesc
           ,@cmpTelIIDesc
           ,@cmpFaxDesc
           ,@cmpAddress
           ,@cmpAddressDesc
           ,@cmpInteresting
           ,@cmpSpecialPoint
           ,@cmpDescription
           ,@cmpSpecialWordForCI
           ,@cmpInquiry
	 FROM dbo.Languages LN 
	 WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID	
	 /*RZ961028- check deactive lang*/
	 AND LN.IsActive =1
	/******* Insert to multi group table ********/
	IF(@cmpMultiGroups IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @GroupId INT
		DECLARE CrsrMultiGroup Cursor For
			SELECT splitdata 
			FROM dbo.fnSplitString(@cmpMultiGroups, '|')		
		/**/
		OPEN CrsrMultiGroup
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrMultiGroup INTO @GroupId
		WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
		BEGIN
	 		INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompanyMultiGroups]
			   (
				[CompanyID]
			   ,[CompanyGroupsMultiID]
			   ,[UserId]
			   ,[Year1]
			   ,[Month1]
			   ,[RegisterDate]
			   ,[RegisterDatePersian]
			   ,[AppType]
			   ,[LanguageID]
			   ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
			   ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
			   ,[FirstRegisterDate]
				)
			VALUES
			   (
				@InsertCmpId
			   ,CAST(@GroupId AS INT)
			   ,@UserId
			   ,@Year1
			   ,@Month1
			   ,@RegisterDate
			   ,@RegisterDatePersian
			   ,@AppType
			   ,@DefaultLanguageID
			   ,@cmpFirstRegisterUserId
			   ,@cmpFirstRegisterDatePersian
			   ,@cmpFirstRegisterDate
			   )			
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrMultiGroup INTO @GroupId
		END
		CLOSE CrsrMultiGroup
		DEALLOCATE CrsrMultiGroup
	END		
	/******* Insert to Logo table ***************/	
	IF(@CompanyLogo IS NOT NULL)	
	BEGIN
		INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmCompanyLoGo]
           ([CompanyId]
           ,[CompanyLogo]
           ,[UserId]
           ,[Year1]
           ,[Month1]
           ,[RegisterDate]
           ,[RegisterDatePersian]
           ,[AppType]
           ,[LanguageID]
           ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
           ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
           ,[FirstRegisterDate])
		VALUES
           (@InsertCmpId
           ,@CompanyLogo
           ,@UserId
           ,@Year1
           ,@Month1
           ,@RegisterDate
           ,@RegisterDatePersian
           ,@AppType
           ,@DefaultLanguageID
           ,@cmpFirstRegisterUserId
           ,@cmpFirstRegisterDatePersian
           ,@cmpFirstRegisterDate)	           
	END		 	 	 
END

IF (@ErrorNumber > 0)
BEGIN 
	RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)
	ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
 	SELECT 0
END 
ELSE
BEGIN
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
	SELECT 1
END	
/**/
END





GO

	@cnvsUserId INT = NULL,
	@cnvsReferUserId INT = NULL,
	@cnvsReferDesc NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
	@cnvsStatusId INT = NULL,
	@cnvsPriorityId INT = NULL,
	@cnvsRemindDate NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@cnvsRemindTime NVARCHAR(15) = NULL,
	@cnvsRemindDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
	@cnvsRemindOk BIT = NULL,
	@cnvsIsBookMark BIT = NULL,
	@cnvsFirstRegisterUserId INT = NULL,
	@cnvsFirstRegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,

	@cnvsCoordinateLongitude NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
	@cnvsCoordinateLastDate DATETIME = NULL,
	@cnvsCoordinateLastDatePersian VARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@cnvsEventPolicyNeedCheck BIT = NULL ,
	@TmpUserMultiUserText NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL ,
	/*RZ971103- Add field*/

	@cnvsDoingTime TIME(7) = NULL,
	@cnvsIsCopy BIT = NULL,
	/*RZ971103- END*/
	@cnvsCallHistoryIds NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL,/*AI14010124*/
	/*AI14010414*/
	@cnvsDoingTimeSec BIGINT = NULL,
	@cnvsStatusProcessKindId INT = NULL,
	@cnvsStatusGroupId INT = NULL,
	@isCloseTicket BIT = NULL,
	/*AI14010414-End*/
	/*AI14010602*/
	@cnvsEmailEventPolicyNeedCheck BIT = NULL,
	@cnvsAvoidSendSms BIT = NULL,
	@cnvsAvoidSendEmail BIT = NULL,
	@prjTicketStop BIT = NULL,
	/*AI14010602-End*/
	@cnvsHappyVote INT = NULL, /*AI14010609*/
	@cnvsStatusResultId INT = NULL, /*AI14010908*/
	@cnvsKnowledgeTankIds NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL, /*AI14020310*/
	/*AI14020429*/
	@cnvsDoingTimeFrom TIME(7) = NULL,
	@cnvsDoingTimeTo TIME(7) = NULL,
	/*AI14020429-End*/

	@cnvsIsCopyRemindDate BIT = NULL,
	@cnvsIsCopyRemindDateForAllarm BIT = NULL
	/*GHO14030129-End*/

IF(@ConversationList IS NOT NULL AND @ConversationList <> '')
BEGIN	
WHILE LEN(@ConversationList) > 0 AND (@ConversationList IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	/*@ConversationList = N'cnvdDesc=desc1|&|cnvsTopicId=1|&||^#^|cnvdDesc=desc2|&|cnvsTopicId=2|&||^#^|'*/
    IF PATINDEX('%|^#^|%', @ConversationList) > 0
    BEGIN
        SET @CnvsAllFields = SUBSTRING(@ConversationList,
                                    0,
                                    PATINDEX('%|^#^|%', @ConversationList))
        /*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
        /*For example => @CnvsAllFields = cnvdDesc=desc1|&|cnvsTopicId=1|&|*/
        /*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
        /*---------------------------- BEGIN Fill fields  --------------------------------*/
        /*BEGIN Conversation*/
        DECLARE @CnAllFields NVARCHAR(MAX)
        SET @CnAllFields = @CnvsAllFields
        
        /**************** set null field **************/
         
        SET @ConversationID  = NULL
		SET @cnvsProjectId  = NULL
		SET @cnvsCompanyId  = NULL
		SET @cnvsDate  = NULL
		SET @cnvsDay  = NULL
		SET @cnvsTime  = NULL

		SET @cnvsReferUserId  = NULL
