		SET @cnvsCoordinateLongitude  = NULL
		SET @cnvsCoordinateLastDate  = NULL
		SET @cnvsCoordinateLastDatePersian  = NULL
		SET @cnvsEventPolicyNeedCheck  = NULL 
		SET @TmpUserMultiUserText  = NULL    
		/*RZ971103- Add field*/ 	
		SET @cnvsFOCmpCanSee = NULL
		SET @cnvsIsTicket = NULL	
		SET @cnvsTicketIsRead = NULL
		SET @cnvsDoingTime = NULL
		SET @cnvsIsCopy  = NULL
		/*RZ971103- END*/   
		SET @cnvsCallHistoryIds = NULL /*AI14010124*/
		/*AI14010414*/
		SET @cnvsDoingTimeSec = NULL
		SET @cnvsStatusProcessKindId = NULL
		SET @cnvsStatusGroupId = NULL
		SET @isCloseTicket = NULL
		/*AI14010414-End*/
		/*AI14010602*/
		SET @cnvsEmailEventPolicyNeedCheck = NULL
		SET @cnvsAvoidSendSms = NULL
		SET @cnvsAvoidSendEmail = NULL
		SET @prjTicketStop = NULL
		/*AI14010602-End*/
		SET @cnvsHappyVote = NULL /*AI14010609*/
		SET @cnvsStatusResultId = NULL /*AI14010908*/
		SET @cnvsKnowledgeTankIds = NULL /*AI14020310*/
		/*GHO14030129*/
		SET @cnvsIsCopyRemindDate =NULL
		SET @cnvsIsCopyRemindDateForAllarm = NULL
		/*GHO14030129-End*/
        /**/
        WHILE LEN(@CnAllFields) > 0
		BEGIN
			IF PATINDEX('%|#|%', @CnAllFields) > 0
			BEGIN
				SET @CnvsField = SUBSTRING(@CnAllFields,
											0,
											PATINDEX('%|#|%', @CnAllFields))
				/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
				/*Get parameter name and split value form parameter*/
				/*For example => cnvsTopicId=102 => cnvsTopicId= => cnvsTopicId => Compare => value*/
				DECLARE @CnvsFieldValue NVARCHAR(MAX)
				DECLARE @CnvsFieldName NVARCHAR(200)
				SET @CnvsFieldName = SUBSTRING(@CnvsField, 1, CHARINDEX('=',@CnvsField)-1)                                    
				SET @CnvsFieldValue = SUBSTRING(@CnvsField, CHARINDEX('=', @CnvsField)+1, Len(@CnvsField))
				/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
				/*---------------------------- BEGIN Fill variabele ------------------------------*/
				/*BEGIN Conversation*/
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @CnvsFieldName = 'ConversationID'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @ConversationID = CASE WHEN @CnvsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CnvsFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @CnvsFieldName = 'cnvsProjectId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @cnvsProjectId = CASE WHEN @CnvsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CnvsFieldValue AS INT) END

					/*RZ971222- Add check access to cmp and project*/
					IF(@cnvsProjectId IS NOT NULL)
					BEGIN
						DECLARE @prjIdFound INT 
										
						SET @prjIdFound = (SELECT ProjectID 
											FROM dbo.CrmProjects P
											WHERE ProjectID = @cnvsProjectId		
											AND prjTitle NOT IN 
											(SELECT ForbiddenProjectTitle 
											FROM CrmProjectsForbiddenForUser
											WHERE CrmUserId = @UserID))
						
						IF(@prjIdFound IS NULL)
						BEGIN

							DECLARE @prjTitle NVARCHAR(2000)
							SET @prjTitle = (SELECT
												CASE  WHEN (@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
													THEN (SELECT prjTitle
													FROM dbo.CrmProjects P WHERE P.ProjectID = @cnvsProjectId)
													ELSE (SELECT prjTitle
														  FROM dbo.CrmProjects_Lang P WHERE P.ProjectID = @cnvsProjectId AND P.LanguageID = @LanguageID)
												END
											 )

							SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=PrjAccess|' + CAST(@ConversationID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '|CNVSID|' + ISNULL(@prjTitle,'') + '|Msg=PrjAccess|'
						END
					END

					/*RZ971222- END*/
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @CnvsFieldName = 'cnvsCompanyId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @cnvsCompanyId = CASE WHEN @CnvsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CnvsFieldValue AS INT) END

					/*RZ971222- Add check access to cmp and project*/
					IF(@cnvsCompanyId IS NOT NULL)
					BEGIN
						DECLARE @IsAccessCompanyId BIT = 0

						EXEC dbo.MobspCheckCmpDataPermissionForUser 	
						@Mode = 8,
						@Id = @cnvsCompanyId,
						@CrmUserId = @UserId,
						@IsAccess = @IsAccessCompanyId OUTPUT
				
						IF(@IsAccessCompanyId = 0)
						BEGIN
							DECLARE @CmpName NVARCHAR(2000)
							SET @CmpName = (SELECT
												CASE  WHEN (@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
													THEN (SELECT  
															ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId),'') + ' ' +
															ISNULL(cmpName,'')
													FROM dbo.CrmCompany C WHERE C.CompanyID = @cnvsCompanyId)
													ELSE (SELECT  
															ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId AND CT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'') + ' ' +
															ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CL WHERE CL.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CL.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')
														  FROM dbo.CrmCompany C WHERE C.CompanyID = @cnvsCompanyId)
												END
											 )
							
							SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=CompanyAccess|' + CAST(@ConversationID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '|CNVSID|' + ISNULL(@CmpName,'') + '|Msg=CompanyAccess|'
						END	 
					END
					/*RZ971222- END*/
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @CnvsFieldName = 'cnvsDate'
				BEGIN
					SET @cnvsDate = CASE WHEN @CnvsFieldValue = '' THEN NULL ELSE REPLACE(@CnvsFieldValue, '/', '') END							
					SET @cnvsDateTime = CASE WHEN @cnvsDate IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@cnvsDate, null) END			 	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @CnvsFieldName = 'cnvsDay'
				BEGIN
					SET @cnvsDay = CASE WHEN @CnvsFieldValue = '' OR @CnvsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @CnvsFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @CnvsFieldName = 'cnvsTime'
				BEGIN
					SET @cnvsTime = CASE WHEN @CnvsFieldValue = '' OR @CnvsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @CnvsFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @CnvsFieldName = 'cnvsTopicId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @cnvsTopicId = CASE WHEN @CnvsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CnvsFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @CnvsFieldName = 'cnvsSubject'
				BEGIN
					SET @cnvsSubject = CASE WHEN @CnvsFieldValue = '' OR @CnvsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @CnvsFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @CnvsFieldName = 'cnvsDesc'
				BEGIN
					SET @cnvsDesc = CASE WHEN @CnvsFieldValue = '' OR @CnvsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @CnvsFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @CnvsFieldName = 'cnvsPersonId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @cnvsPersonId = CASE WHEN @CnvsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CnvsFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @CnvsFieldName = 'cnvsUserId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @cnvsUserId = CASE WHEN @CnvsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CnvsFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @CnvsFieldName = 'cnvsReferUserId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @cnvsReferUserId = CASE WHEN @CnvsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CnvsFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @CnvsFieldName = 'cnvsReferDesc'
				BEGIN
					SET @cnvsReferDesc = CASE WHEN @CnvsFieldValue = '' OR @CnvsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @CnvsFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @CnvsFieldName = 'cnvsStatusId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @cnvsStatusId = CASE WHEN @CnvsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CnvsFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @CnvsFieldName = 'cnvsPriorityId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @cnvsPriorityId = CASE WHEN @CnvsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CnvsFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @CnvsFieldName = 'cnvsRemindDate'
				BEGIN
					SET @cnvsRemindDate = CASE WHEN @CnvsFieldValue = '' THEN NULL ELSE REPLACE(@CnvsFieldValue, '/', '') END		
					/*RZ961216- Delete Time from cnvsRemindDateTime*/
					SET @cnvsRemindDateTime = CASE WHEN @cnvsRemindDate IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@cnvsRemindDate, null) END			 	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @CnvsFieldName = 'cnvsRemindTime'
				BEGIN
					SET @cnvsRemindTime = CASE WHEN @CnvsFieldValue = '' OR @CnvsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @CnvsFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @CnvsFieldName = 'cnvsRemindOk'
				BEGIN
					SET @cnvsRemindOk = CASE WHEN @CnvsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL 
										WHEN @CnvsFieldValue = 'false' THEN 0 
										ELSE 1 END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @CnvsFieldName = 'cnvsIsBookMark'
				BEGIN
					SET @cnvsIsBookMark = CASE WHEN @CnvsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL 
										WHEN @CnvsFieldValue = 'false' THEN 0 
										ELSE 1 END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @CnvsFieldName = 'cnvsFirstRegisterUserId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @cnvsFirstRegisterUserId = CASE WHEN @CnvsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@CnvsFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @CnvsFieldName = 'cnvsFirstRegisterDatePersian'
				BEGIN
					SET @cnvsFirstRegisterDatePersian = CASE WHEN @CnvsFieldValue = '' OR @CnvsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE REPLACE(@CnvsFieldValue, '/', '') END							
					SET @cnvsFirstRegisterDate = CASE WHEN @cnvsFirstRegisterDatePersian IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@cnvsFirstRegisterDatePersian, null) END			 	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @CnvsFieldName = 'cnvsRemindDateForAllarm'
				BEGIN
					SET @cnvsRemindDateForAllarm = CASE WHEN @CnvsFieldValue = '' OR @CnvsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE REPLACE(@CnvsFieldValue, '/', '') END							
					SET @cnvsRemindDateTimeForAllarm = CASE WHEN @cnvsRemindDateForAllarm IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@cnvsRemindDateForAllarm, null) END			 	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @CnvsFieldName = 'ApiId'
				BEGIN
					SET @ApiId = CASE WHEN @CnvsFieldValue = '' OR @CnvsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @CnvsFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @CnvsFieldName = 'cnvsCoordinateLatitude'
				BEGIN
					SET @cnvsCoordinateLatitude = CASE WHEN @CnvsFieldValue = '' OR @CnvsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @CnvsFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @CnvsFieldName = 'cnvsCoordinateLongitude'
				BEGIN
					SET @cnvsCoordinateLongitude = CASE WHEN @CnvsFieldValue = '' OR @CnvsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @CnvsFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				/*IF @CnvsFieldName = 'cnvsCoordinateLastDatePersian'

														  (prjTitle + ISNULL(' ' + prjPostFix, '')) AS prjFullTitle		 	  
													FROM dbo.CrmProjects P
													WHERE P.ProjectID = @PrjIdInDb)																																													
							END
							ELSE
							BEGIN
								SET @CompanyFullName = (SELECT 
															(ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle_Lang CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleId = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = @LanguageID), '') + ' '+ 
																ISNULL((SELECT [cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.[CompanyID] = C.[CompanyID]  AND CLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),C.[cmpName])) CompanyName																			 
														FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] C
														WHERE C.[CompanyID] = @CompanyID)
								/**/						
								SET @PersonFullName  = (SELECT
															  (ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle_Lang ct WHERE ct.PersonTitleID = prsTitleId AND ct.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'') 
																	+ ISNULL(' ' + (SELECT prsLName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang PLN WHERE PLN.CompanyPersonID = P.CompanyPersonID  AND PLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID) , '') 
																	+ ISNULL(' ' + (SELECT prsFName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang PLN WHERE PLN.CompanyPersonID = P.CompanyPersonID  AND PLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID) , '')) 
															  AS prsFullName
														
														FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons  P
														WHERE CompanyPersonID = @PrsIdInDb)
								/**/
								SET @ProjectTitle = (SELECT 
														  (ISNULL((SELECT [prjTitle] FROM dbo.CrmProjects_Lang PLN WHERE PLN.ProjectID = P.ProjectID  AND PLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),P.[prjTitle]) 
																+ ISNULL(' ' + (SELECT prjPostFix FROM dbo.CrmProjects_Lang PLN WHERE PLN.ProjectID = P.ProjectID  AND PLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID), '')) 
														  AS prjFullTitle		  		  
													FROM dbo.CrmProjects P
													WHERE P.ProjectID = @PrjIdInDb)														
							END*/
							/**************** Get multi user ids & names ************/														
							/*TmpUserMultiUserText = '1|2^amin|ali' => id of users |1|2 + ^ + name of users |amin|ali*/
							/*DECLARE @UserMultiUserText NVARCHAR(MAX), @UserMultiUserIds NVARCHAR(MAX), @UserMultiUserName NVARCHAR(MAX)
							IF(@TmpUserMultiUserText IS NOT NULL AND @TmpUserMultiUserText <> '')
							BEGIN
								SET @UserMultiUserIds = REPLACE((SUBSTRING(@TmpUserMultiUserText,0, PATINDEX('%^%', @TmpUserMultiUserText))),'|',',')
								SET @UserMultiUserName = REPLACE((SUBSTRING(@TmpUserMultiUserText,PATINDEX('%^%', @TmpUserMultiUserText) + 1, LEN(@TmpUserMultiUserText))),'|',',')
							END AI14010908*/
							DECLARE @UserMultiUserIds NVARCHAR(MAX)
							SET @UserMultiUserIds = @TmpUserMultiUserText
							/************************* needErja *********************/																												
							DECLARE @NeedErja BIT = 0
							IF(@cnvsReferUserId <> @UserId)
							BEGIN
								SET @NeedErja = 1
								/*SET @NeedErjaRefmsgBody = REPLACE((REPLACE((REPLACE(@NeedErjaRefmsgBody,'{CMP}',@CompanyFullName)),'{PRS}',@PersonFullName)),'{PRJ}',@ProjectTitle)
								IF(@UserMultiUserName IS NOT NULL AND @UserMultiUserName <> '')
								BEGIN
									SET @MsgConversationErjaMultiUser = REPLACE(@MsgConversationErjaMultiUser, '{MultiUser}', @UserMultiUserName)
									/*RZ961002-Edit*/
									SET @NeedErjaRefmsgBody =  REPLACE(@NeedErjaRefmsgBody, '{MultiUser}', @MsgConversationErjaMultiUser)
								END
								ELSE
									SET @NeedErjaRefmsgBody =  REPLACE(@NeedErjaRefmsgBody, '{MultiUser}', '')
							
								SET @NeedErjaRefmsgBody = REPLACE(@NeedErjaRefmsgBody , '{CNVSDESC}',@cnvsDesc)
								IF(@cnvsReferDesc IS NOT NULL AND @cnvsReferDesc <> '')
								BEGIN
									SET @MsgConversationErjaReferDescription = REPLACE(@MsgConversationErjaReferDescription, '{CNVSREFERDESC}', @cnvsReferDesc)
									/*RZ961002-Edit*/
									SET @NeedErjaRefmsgBody =  REPLACE(@NeedErjaRefmsgBody, '{CNVSREFERDESC}', @MsgConversationErjaReferDescription)
								END   
								ELSE
									SET @NeedErjaRefmsgBody =  REPLACE(@NeedErjaRefmsgBody, '{CNVSREFERDESC}', '') */                       									                           									
							END
							/******************* needErjaMulti **********************/
							DECLARE @NeedErjaMulti BIT = 0
							IF(@TmpUserMultiUserText IS NOT NULL AND @TmpUserMultiUserText <> '')
							BEGIN
								SET @NeedErjaMulti = 1
								/*SET @NeedErjaMultiIntmsgBody  = REPLACE((REPLACE((REPLACE(@NeedErjaMultiIntmsgBody,'{CMP}',@CompanyFullName)),'{PRS}',@PersonFullName)),'{PRJ}',@ProjectTitle)
								SET @NeedErjaMultiIntmsgBody = REPLACE(@NeedErjaMultiIntmsgBody, '{MultiUser}', ISNULL(@UserMultiUserName,''))
								SET @NeedErjaMultiIntmsgBody = REPLACE(@NeedErjaMultiIntmsgBody , '{CNVSDESC}',@cnvsDesc)
								IF(@cnvsReferDesc IS NOT NULL AND @cnvsReferDesc <> '')
								BEGIN
									SET @MsgConversationErjaReferDescription = REPLACE(@MsgConversationErjaReferDescription, '{CNVSREFERDESC}', @cnvsReferDesc)
									/*RZ961002-Edit*/
									SET @NeedErjaMultiIntmsgBody =  REPLACE(@NeedErjaMultiIntmsgBody, '{CNVSREFERDESC}', @MsgConversationErjaReferDescription)                           									
								END 
								ELSE
									SET @NeedErjaMultiIntmsgBody =  REPLACE(@NeedErjaMultiIntmsgBody, '{CNVSREFERDESC}', '')  */                         									 																														
							END
							/******************* needSendErjaAllarm **********************/
							DECLARE @NeedSendErjaAllarm BIT = 0
							/******* Exec MobspSelectCompanyConversationLastStatus *******/
							/*DECLARE @LastCnvsReferUserId INT																													
							Exec dbo.MobspSelectCompanyConversationLastStatus
							@CompanyId = @CompanyID, 
							@ProjectId = @PrjIdInDb,
							@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
							@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID,
							@CnvsReferUserId  = @LastCnvsReferUserId OUTPUT	*/						
																																														
							IF(@LastCnvsReferUserId IS NOT NULL)
							BEGIN
								/*** Exec MobspSelectDisableReceiveCNVSInfoAllarmStatus ***/
								DECLARE @DisableReceiveCNVSInfoAllarm BIT 																														
								EXEC dbo.MobspSelectDisableReceiveCNVSInfoAllarmStatus  
								@RefUserId  = @LastCnvsReferUserId ,
								@DisableReceiveCNVSInfoAllarm = @DisableReceiveCNVSInfoAllarm OUTPUT
																						
								IF(@LastCnvsReferUserId <> @cnvsReferUserId AND
								   @LastCnvsReferUserId <> @cnvsUserId AND
								   ISNULL(@DisableReceiveCNVSInfoAllarm,0) <> 1 AND
								   ISNULL(@cnvsIsTicket, 0) = 0
								   )
								BEGIN
									SET @NeedSendErjaAllarm = 1
									/*SET @NeedSendErjaAllarmRefmsgBody = REPLACE((REPLACE((REPLACE(@NeedSendErjaAllarmRefmsgBody,'{CMP}',@CompanyFullName)),'{PRS}',@PersonFullName)),'{PRJ}',@ProjectTitle)
									SET @NeedSendErjaAllarmRefmsgBody = REPLACE(@NeedSendErjaAllarmRefmsgBody, '{MultiUser}', ISNULL(@UserMultiUserName,''))
									
									SET @NeedSendErjaAllarmRefmsgBody = REPLACE(@NeedSendErjaAllarmRefmsgBody , '{CNVSDESC}',@cnvsDesc)
									IF(@cnvsReferDesc IS NOT NULL AND @cnvsReferDesc <> '')
									BEGIN
										SET @MsgConversationErjaReferDescription = REPLACE(@MsgConversationErjaReferDescription, '{CNVSREFERDESC}', @cnvsReferDesc)
										/*RZ961002-Edit*/
										SET @NeedSendErjaAllarmRefmsgBody =  REPLACE(@NeedSendErjaAllarmRefmsgBody, '{CNVSREFERDESC}', @MsgConversationErjaReferDescription)
									END  
									ELSE
										SET @NeedSendErjaAllarmRefmsgBody = REPLACE(@NeedSendErjaAllarmRefmsgBody, '{CNVSREFERDESC}', '')   */                        									                           									                           									
								END															
							END
						
						
							/*************************************************************/
							/*RZ970420- Add multi language in internalmessage*/
							EXEC dbo.MobspGetMultiLangMessageForCnvsRefer 
								@UserId = @UserId, 
								@LinkedType = @LinkedType,
								@ReferMultiUserIdsSelected =  @UserMultiUserIds,
								@CompanyId = @CompanyID,
								@CompanyPersonId = @PrsIdInDb,

								@cnvsDesc = @cnvsDesc,
								@cnvsReferDesc = @cnvsReferDesc,
								@CmpNotAccess = @CmpNotAccess,

								@NeedErjaMultiIntmsgBody = @NeedErjaMultiIntmsgBody OUTPUT,
								@NeedSendErjaAllarmRefmsgBody = @NeedSendErjaAllarmRefmsgBody OUTPUT
							/*RZ970420- Add multi language in internalmessage End*/
						
							/************** Exec MobspInsertCrmConversation **************/
							DECLARE @CurrTime NVARCHAR(8), @FirstRegisterDateAddSecond DATETIME, @ResultRunCnvsEventPolicyForSms INT															
							SET @CurrTime = (SELECT CONVERT(NVARCHAR(8), getdate(), 108))	
							/*SET @FirstRegisterDateAddSecond =(SELECT DATEADD(ss,-7, @cnvsFirstRegisterDate)) AI14010621*/
							SET @FirstRegisterDateAddSecond =(SELECT DATEADD(ss,-7, @RegisterDate))
							/**/																																																			
							SET @InsertCnvsId  = 0
							SET @ResultRunCnvsEventPolicyForSms = 0
							EXEC dbo.MobspInsertCrmConversation 
							@CnvsProjectId = @PrjIdInDb,
							@CnvsCompanyId = @CompanyID,
							@CnvsDate = @cnvsDate,
							@CnvsDay = @cnvsDay,
							@CnvsTime = @cnvsTime,
							@CnvsDateTime = @cnvsDateTime,
							@CnvsTopicId = @cnvsTopicId,
							@CnvsSubject = @cnvsSubject,
							@CnvsDesc = @cnvsDesc,
							@CnvsPersonId = @PrsIdInDb, 
							@CnvsUserId = @cnvsUserId, 
							@CnvsReferUserId = @cnvsReferUserId, 
							@CnvsReferDesc = @cnvsReferDesc, 
							@CnvsStatusId = @cnvsStatusId,
							@CnvsPriorityId = @cnvsPriorityId, 
							@CnvsRemindDate = @cnvsRemindDate,
							@CnvsRemindTime = @cnvsRemindTime,
							@CnvsRemindDateTime = @cnvsRemindDateTime,
							@CnvsRemindDateForAllarm = @cnvsRemindDateForAllarm,
							@CnvsRemindDateTimeForAllarm = @cnvsRemindDateTimeForAllarm,
							@CnvsRemindOk = @cnvsRemindOk,
							@CnvsIsBookMark = @cnvsIsBookMark,
							@Year1 = @Year1,
							@Month1 = @Month1,
							@UserId = @UserId,
							@RegisterDate = @RegisterDate, 
							@RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
							@FirstRegisterUserId = @cnvsFirstRegisterUserId,
							@FirstRegisterDate = @RegisterDate, /*AI14010621 - @cnvsFirstRegisterDate*/
							@FirstRegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian, /*AI14010621 - @cnvsFirstRegisterDatePersian*/
							@AppType = @AppType,
							@LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID,
							@TmpUserMultiUserText = @UserMultiUserIds,
							@ApiId = @ApiId,
							@NeedErja = @NeedErja, 
							@NeedErjaRefmsgSubj = @NeedErjaRefmsgSubj, 
							@NeedErjaRefmsgBody = @NeedErjaRefmsgBody,
							@NeedErjaMulti = @NeedErjaMulti, 
							@NeedErjaMultiIntmsgSubject = @NeedErjaMultiIntmsgSubject,
							@NeedErjaMultiIntmsgBody = @NeedErjaMultiIntmsgBody,
							@NeedSendErjaAllarm = @NeedSendErjaAllarm,
							@NeedSendErjaAllarmRefmsgSubj = @NeedSendErjaAllarmRefmsgSubj,
							@NeedSendErjaAllarmRefmsgBody = @NeedSendErjaAllarmRefmsgBody,
							@LastCnvsReferUserId = @LastCnvsReferUserId,
							@ConversationErjaShowText = @ConversationErjaShowText,
							@CurrTime = @CurrTime,
							@InternalMessageYear = @CurrYear,
							@FirstRegisterDateAddSecond = @FirstRegisterDateAddSecond,
							@CnvsCoordinateLatitude = @cnvsCoordinateLatitude,
							@CnvsCoordinateLongitude = @cnvsCoordinateLongitude,
							@CnvsCoordinateLastDate = @cnvsCoordinateLastDate, /*AI14010621 - @cnvsCoordinateLastDate*/
							@CnvsCoordinateLastDatePersian = @cnvsCoordinateLastDatePersian, /*AI14010621 - @cnvsCoordinateLastDatePersian*/
							@EventPolicyNeedCheck = @cnvsEventPolicyNeedCheck,
							/*RZ971103- Add field*/ 	
							@CnvsFOCmpCanSee = @cnvsFOCmpCanSee,
							@CnvsIsTicket = @cnvsIsTicket,	
							@CnvsTicketIsRead = @cnvsTicketIsRead,
							@CnvsDoingTime = @cnvsDoingTime,
							@CnvsIsCopy = @cnvsIsCopy,
							/*RZ971103- END*/	
							@CnvsCallHistoryIds = @cnvsCallHistoryIds, /*AI14010124*/
							/*AI14010414*/
							@CnvsDoingTimeSec = @cnvsDoingTimeSec,
							@CnvsStatusProcessKindId = @cnvsStatusProcessKindId,
							@CnvsStatusGroupId = @cnvsStatusGroupId,
							@IsCloseTicket = @isCloseTicket,
							/*AI14010414*/
							/*AI14010602*/
							@AvoidSendSms = @cnvsAvoidSendSms,
							@AvoidSendEmail = @cnvsAvoidSendEmail,
							@EmailEventPolicyNeedCheck = @cnvsEmailEventPolicyNeedCheck,
							@PrjTicketStop = @prjTicketStop,
							/*AI14010602-End*/
							@CnvsHappyVote = @cnvsHappyVote, /*AI14010609*/
							@CnvsStatusResultId = @cnvsStatusResultId, /*AI14010908*/
							/*AI14010909*/
							@SurveyMainInternalLink = @SurveyMainInternalLink,
							@SurveyMainExtrenalLink = @SurveyMainExtrenalLink,
							@SurveyMainExtrenalLinkLogin = @SurveyMainExtrenalLinkLogin,
							@EventConversationErjaShowText = @EventConversationErjaShowText,
							@EventErjaRefmsg = @EventErjaRefmsg,
							/*AI14010909-End*/
							@CnvsKnowledgeTankIds = @cnvsKnowledgeTankIds, /*AI14020310*/
							/*AI14020429*/
							@CnvsDoingTimeFrom = @cnvsDoingTimeFrom,
							@CnvsDoingTimeTo = @cnvsDoingTimeTo,
							/*AI14020429-End*/
							@CnvsIdInserted = @InsertCnvsId OUTPUT,
							@ResultRunCnvsEventPolicyForSms = @ResultRunCnvsEventPolicyForSms OUTPUT,
							/*GHO14030129*/
							@cnvsIsCopyRemindDate = @cnvsIsCopyRemindDate,
							@cnvsIsCopyRemindDateForAllarm =@cnvsIsCopyRemindDateForAllarm
							/*GHO14030129-End*/
							IF(@ResultRunCnvsEventPolicyForSms = -1)
								SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage  + '<CNVSID>' + CAST(@ConversationID AS NVARCHAR(10))+ '<\CNVSID>' + 'Msg=6'	/*not insert sms */					
																						 																																										     								     				
							/*************** Ids inserted ********/				
							SET @InsertedConversationIds = @InsertedConversationIds + CAST(@ConversationID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ',' + CAST(@InsertCnvsId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '|'   /*cnvsIdMob,cnvsIdDb*/
							/*RZ970502- Add check ForcedStatus*/
						END
						ELSE
						BEGIN
							SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '<CNVS><CNVSID>' + CAST(@ConversationID AS NVARCHAR(10))+ '<\CNVSID>' + '<ForcedStatus>' + @ConversationForcedStatus +'<\ForcedStatus><\CNVS>'	/*ForcedStatus */					
						END
						/*RZ970502- Add check ForcedStatus END*/								
					END
					ELSE
						SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage  + '<CNVSID>' + CAST(@ConversationID AS NVARCHAR(10))+ '<\CNVSID>' + '<PRJID>' + CAST(@cnvsProjectId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '<\PRJID>' + 'Msg=5'	/*not insert cnvsProject */																														
				END
				ELSE
					SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage  + '<CNVSID>' + CAST(@ConversationID AS NVARCHAR(10))+ '<\CNVSID>' + '<PRSID>' + CAST(@cnvsPersonId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '<\PRSID>' + 'Msg=3'	/*not insert cnvsperson */																
			END
			ELSE /*when @CompanyTmpID <> @cnvsCompanyId that come from mobile*/
				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage  + '<CNVSID>' + CAST(@ConversationID AS NVARCHAR(10))+ '<\CNVSID>' + 'Msg=1'
		/**/	
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrCnvs INTO @ConversationID, @cnvsProjectId, @cnvsCompanyId, @cnvsDate, @cnvsDay, @cnvsTime, @cnvsDateTime,
				@cnvsTopicId, @cnvsSubject, @cnvsDesc, @cnvsPersonId, @cnvsUserId, @cnvsReferUserId, @cnvsReferDesc,
				@cnvsStatusId, @cnvsPriorityId, @cnvsRemindDate, @cnvsRemindTime, @cnvsRemindDateTime, @cnvsRemindOk,
				@cnvsIsBookMark, @cnvsFirstRegisterUserId, @cnvsFirstRegisterDatePersian, @cnvsFirstRegisterDate,		
				@cnvsRemindDateForAllarm, @cnvsRemindDateTimeForAllarm, @ApiId, @cnvsCoordinateLatitude,
				@cnvsCoordinateLongitude, @cnvsCoordinateLastDate, @cnvsCoordinateLastDatePersian, @cnvsEventPolicyNeedCheck, @TmpUserMultiUserText,
				/*RZ971103- Add field*/ 	
				@cnvsFOCmpCanSee, @cnvsIsTicket, @cnvsTicketIsRead, @cnvsDoingTime, @cnvsIsCopy,
				/*RZ971103- END*/
				@cnvsCallHistoryIds /*AI14010124*/
				/*AI14010414*/
				,@cnvsDoingTimeSec, @cnvsStatusProcessKindId, @cnvsStatusGroupId, @isCloseTicket
				/*AI14010414-End*/
				,@cnvsEmailEventPolicyNeedCheck, @cnvsAvoidSendSms, @cnvsAvoidSendEmail, @prjTicketStop /*AI14010602*/ , @cnvsHappyVote /*AI14010609*/
				,@cnvsStatusResultId /*AI14010908*/, @cnvsKnowledgeTankIds /*AI14020310*/ /*AI14020429*/,@cnvsDoingTimeFrom , @cnvsDoingTimeTo/*AI14020429*/
				,@cnvsIsCopyRemindDate /*GHO14030129*/,@cnvsIsCopyRemindDateForAllarm /*GHO14030129*/
	END
	CLOSE CrsrCnvs
	DEALLOCATE CrsrCnvs	

END
/**/
IF (@@ERROR > 0)
BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert Conversation' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
ELSE 
COMMIT TRAN
/**/
/**/
IF (@ErrorNumber > 0)
BEGIN 
	RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)
	ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
 	/*SELECT 0*/
	SELECT '' [Status],'' cnvsUser, '' cnvsReferUser, 0 cnvsReferUserId	
 	SET @Result = 0
END 
ELSE
BEGIN
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
	/*SELECT 1*/
	SET @Result = 1
END	
/**/
END










GO
-- [dbo].[MobspInsertQuickDocItem] | References permission/temp-filter machinery






/*AI14020604*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspInsertQuickDocItem]
/*
	EXEC MobspInsertQuickDocItem
*/
(
@DocItemParams NVARCHAR(MAX),
@UserId INT,
@FPId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@AppType VARCHAR(20), 
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL,
@PrdStockTxt NVARCHAR(100),
/**/
@ResErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT,
@ResInsertedDocItemIds NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT,
@ResLinkedErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
	BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
	BEGIN TRY
		SET @ResErrorMessage = ''
		SET @ResLinkedErrorMessage = ''

		/**************************************************************************/
		/***********************************HELPER*********************************/
		/**************************************************************************/

		DECLARE @SysMustFullTracingCode BIT, @PrdMustFullTracingCode BIT, @actLoadTracingCodeFromAllPrd BIT,
				@actCanNotAddNewTracingCode BIT, @tracingPrdId INT, @DocItemTracingExist NVARCHAR(150),
				@OprType INT, @TracingRemainCount FLOAT, @LinkedErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX),
				@dWHId INT, @dWHidI INT

		SELECT
			@SysMustFullTracingCode = [MustFullTracingCode]
		FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting]

		DECLARE @DocItemTracingTmpTbl TABLE(
			[Tracing] NVARCHAR(200)
		)

		DECLARE @InsertLinkedPrdTmpTbl TABLE(
			[Result] INT,
			[ErrorMessage] NVARCHAR(MAX)
		)

		DECLARE @ReturnPrdQuantityTmpTbl TABLE(
			[QuantityRemain] FLOAT,
			[QuantityIn] FLOAT,
			[QuantityOut] FLOAT,
			[PrdId] INT,
			[PrdName] NVARCHAR(1000),
			[RemainCount] FLOAT,
			[NetPreOrderCount] FLOAT,
			[RemainCountAllWH] FLOAT,
			[RemainCurrAndAll] NVARCHAR(200),
			[TracingRemainCount] FLOAT
		)

		DECLARE @diInsertedDocItemID INT, @diDocItemID INT, @diDocId INT, @diPrdId INT, @diQuantity FLOAT, @diCostId INT, @diCost NUMERIC(18,0),
				@diDescription NVARCHAR(MAX), @diIsFix SMALLINT, @diWHId INT, @diNotVisible BIT, @diFCR FLOAT, @diFCRI FLOAT, @diTracing NVARCHAR(200),
				@diPrdUnitId INT, @diQuantityTotal FLOAT, @diPrdUnitIdI INT, @diPrdUnitIRateResult FLOAT, @diPrdNickname NVARCHAR(1000),
				@diInsertLinkedProducts BIT




		BEGIN TRY

		/**************************************************************************/
		/********************************DOC ITEM**********************************/
		/**************************************************************************/

			DECLARE @DocItemsTbl TABLE(
				[InsertedDocItemID] INT,
				[DocItemID] INT,
				[DocId] INT,
				[PrdId] INT,
				[Quantity] FLOAT,
				[CostId] INT,
				[Cost] NUMERIC(18,0),
				[Description] NVARCHAR(MAX),
				[IsFix] SMALLINT,
				[WHId] INT,
				[NotVisible] BIT,
				[FCR] FLOAT,
				[FCRI] FLOAT,
				[Tracing] NVARCHAR(200),
				[PrdUnitId] INT,
				[QuantityTotal] FLOAT,
				[PrdUnitIdI] INT,
				[PrdUnitIRateResult] FLOAT,
				[PrdNickname] NVARCHAR(1000),
				[InsertLinkedProducts] BIT
			)

			IF(ISNULL(@DocItemParams, '') <> '')
			BEGIN
				INSERT INTO @DocItemsTbl
				SELECT
					NULL
					,DIJ.[DocItemID]
					,DIJ.[DocId]
					,DIJ.[PrdId]
					,DIJ.[Quantity]
					,DIJ.[CostId]
					,DIJ.[Cost]
					,DIJ.[Description]
					,DIJ.[IsFix]
					,DIJ.[WHId]
					,DIJ.[NotVisible]
					,DIJ.[FCR]
					,DIJ.[FCRI]
					,DIJ.[Tracing]
					,DIJ.[PrdUnitId]
					,DIJ.[QuantityTotal]
					,DIJ.[PrdUnitIdI]
					,DIJ.[PrdUnitIRateResult]
					,DIJ.[PrdNickname]
					,DIJ.[InsertLinkedProducts]
				FROM OPENJSON(@DocItemParams)
				WITH(
					[DocItemID] INT '$.DocItemID',
					[DocId] INT '$.DocId',
					[PrdId] INT '$.PrdId',
					[Quantity] FLOAT '$.Quantity',
					[CostId] INT '$.CostId',
					[Cost] NUMERIC(18,0) '$.Cost',
					[Description] NVARCHAR(MAX) '$.Description',
					[IsFix] SMALLINT '$.IsFix',
					[WHId] INT '$.WHId',
					[NotVisible] BIT '$.NotVisible',
					[FCR] FLOAT '$.FCR',
					[FCRI] FLOAT '$.FCRI',
					[Tracing] NVARCHAR(200) '$.Tracing',
					[PrdUnitId] INT '$.PrdUnitId',
					[QuantityTotal] FLOAT '$.QuantityTotal',
					[PrdUnitIdI] INT '$.PrdUnitIdI',
					[PrdUnitIRateResult] FLOAT '$.PrdUnitIRateResult',
					[PrdNickname] NVARCHAR(1000) '$.PrdNickname',
					[InsertLinkedProducts] BIT '$.InsertLinkedProducts'
				) AS DIJ
			END

		END TRY
		BEGIN CATCH
			SET @ResErrorMessage = @ResErrorMessage + '#|^|#' + 'ParseData=Error'
			GOTO EndOfScript
		END CATCH

		/**************************************************************************/
		/*****************************Doc Item insert******************************/
		/**************************************************************************/

		DECLARE CrsrDocItem CURSOR LOCAL FOR
		SELECT 
			[InsertedDocItemID], [DocItemID],[DocId],[PrdId],[Quantity],[CostId]
			,[Cost],[Description],[IsFix],[WHId],[NotVisible],[FCR],[FCRI],[Tracing]
			,[PrdUnitId],[QuantityTotal],[PrdUnitIdI],[PrdUnitIRateResult],[PrdNickname]
			,[InsertLinkedProducts]
		FROM @DocItemsTbl OPEN CrsrDocItem FETCH NEXT
		FROM CrsrDocItem INTO @diInsertedDocItemID, @diDocItemID, @diDocId, @diPrdId, @diQuantity, @diCostId, @diCost,
						@diDescription, @diIsFix, @diWHId, @diNotVisible, @diFCR, @diFCRI, @diTracing, @diPrdUnitId, @diQuantityTotal,
						@diPrdUnitIdI, @diPrdUnitIRateResult, @diPrdNickname, @diInsertLinkedProducts
		WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0 BEGIN

			IF(ISNULL(@OprType, 0) = 0)
			BEGIN
				SELECT
					@OprType = (SELECT [OprType] FROM [dbo].[WSDocOpr] WHERE [DocOprID] = 
								(SELECT D.[OprId] FROM [dbo].[WSDoc] D WHERE D.[DocID] = @diDocId))
				SELECT @dWHId = (SELECT D.[WHId] FROM [dbo].[WSDoc] D WHERE D.[DocID] = @diDocId)
				SELECT @dWHidI = (SELECT D.[WHIdI] FROM [dbo].[WSDoc] D WHERE D.[DocID] = @diDocId)
			END

			SELECT
				@PrdMustFullTracingCode = [PrdMustFullTracingCode]
			FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
			WHERE ProductID = @diPrdId

			IF(ISNULL(@diTracing,'') = '')
			BEGIN
				IF(ISNULL(@SysMustFullTracingCode, 0) <> 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @ResErrorMessage = @ResErrorMessage + '#|^|#' + 'Tracing=Force|^|PrdId=' + CAST(@diPrdId AS NVARCHAR(10))
					GOTO EndOfScript
				END
			END
			ELSE IF(ISNULL(@PrdMustFullTracingCode, 0) <> 0)
			BEGIN
				SET @ResErrorMessage = @ResErrorMessage + '#|^|#' + 'Tracing=Force|^|PrdId=' + CAST(@diPrdId AS NVARCHAR(10))
				GOTO EndOfScript
			END

			IF(@OprType = 1)
			BEGIN
				IF(ISNULL(@diTracing,'') <> '')
				BEGIN
					EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckActionForUser]
						@FormName = 'frmDocItemDataEntry',
						@ActionName = 'actLoadTracingCodeFromAllPrd',
						@UserId = @UserId,
						@HasAction = @actLoadTracingCodeFromAllPrd OUTPUT

					EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckActionForUser]
						@FormName = 'frmDocItemDataEntry',
						@ActionName = 'actCanNotAddNewTracingCode',
						@UserId = @UserId,
						@HasAction = @actCanNotAddNewTracingCode OUTPUT

					SET @tracingPrdId = @diPrdId
					IF(@actLoadTracingCodeFromAllPrd = 1)
					BEGIN
						SET @tracingPrdId = 0
					END

					IF(@actCanNotAddNewTracingCode = 1)
					BEGIN
						SET @DocItemTracingExist = NULL
						DELETE @DocItemTracingTmpTbl

						INSERT INTO @DocItemTracingTmpTbl
						EXEC [dbo].[MobspSelectDocItemTracing]
							@Tracing = @diTracing,
							@PrdId = @tracingPrdId

					
						SELECT TOP 1
							@DocItemTracingExist = [Tracing]
						FROM @DocItemTracingTmpTbl

						IF(@DocItemTracingExist IS NULL)
						BEGIN
							SET @ResErrorMessage = @ResErrorMessage + '#|^|#' + 'Tracing=New|^|NewTracing=' + @diTracing + 
								'|^|PrdId=' + CAST(@diPrdId AS NVARCHAR(10))
							GOTO EndOfScript
						END
					END
				END
			END

			IF(@OprType = 2 OR @OprType = 3)
			BEGIN
				SET @TracingRemainCount = NULL
				DELETE @ReturnPrdQuantityTmpTbl

				DECLARE @QtyWhId INT = NULL
				IF(@OprType = 3)
				BEGIN
					SET @QtyWhId = @dWHidI
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					SET @QtyWhId = @dWHId
				END

				INSERT INTO @ReturnPrdQuantityTmpTbl
				EXEC [dbo].[MobspReturnPrdQuantity]
					@PrdId = @diPrdId,
					@FPId = @FPId,
					@WHId = @QtyWhId,
					@CurrId = 0,
					@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
					@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID,
					@FCR = @diFCR,
					@FCRI = @diFCRI,
					@Tracing = @diTracing,
					@QuantityRemain = NULL

				SELECT TOP 1
					@TracingRemainCount = [TracingRemainCount]
				FROM @ReturnPrdQuantityTmpTbl

				IF(ISNULL(@diQuantity, 1) > ISNULL(@TracingRemainCount, 0))
				BEGIN
					SET @ResErrorMessage = @ResErrorMessage + '#|^|#' + 'QtyRemainLimit|^|PrdId=' + CAST(@diPrdId AS NVARCHAR(10))
					GOTO EndOfScript
				END
					
			END


			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSDocItems]
				([DocId]
				,[PrdId]
				,[Quantity]
				,[CostId]
				,[Cost]
				,[Description]
				,[IsFix]
				,[WHId]
				,[UserId]
				,[Month1]
				,[FPId]
				,[RegisterDate]
				,[RegisterDatePersian]
				,[NotVisible]
				,[AppType]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[FirstRegisterUserId]
				,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
				,[FirstRegisterDate]
				,[FCR]
				,[FCRI]
				,[Tracing]
				,[PrdUnitId]
				,[QuantityTotal]
				,[PrdUnitIdI]
				,[PrdUnitIRateResult]
				,[PrdNickname])
			VALUES
				(@diDocId
				,@diPrdId
				,@diQuantity
				,@diCostId
				,@diCost
				,@diDescription
				,@diIsFix
				,@diWHId
				,@UserId
				,@Month1
				,@FPId
				,@RegisterDate
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@diNotVisible
				,@AppType
				,@LanguageID
				,@UserId
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@RegisterDate
				,@diFCR
				,@diFCRI
				,@diTracing
				,@diPrdUnitId
				,@diQuantityTotal
				,@diPrdUnitIdI
				,@diPrdUnitIRateResult
				,@diPrdNickname
				)

				SET @diInsertedDocItemID = SCOPE_IDENTITY()

				UPDATE @DocItemsTbl SET
					[InsertedDocItemID] = @diInsertedDocItemID
				WHERE [DocItemID] = @diDocItemID

				/******* Insert to lang table ********/
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSDocItems_Lang]
					(
					[DocItemID]
					,[LanguageID]
					,[Description]
					,[PrdNickname]
					)
				SELECT
					@diInsertedDocItemID
					,LN.LanguageID
					,@diDescription
					,@diPrdNickname
				FROM dbo.Languages LN 
				WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID	
				AND ISNULL(LN.IsActive,0) = 1
				/******* Linked Products ********/
				IF(ISNULL(@diInsertLinkedProducts, 0) <> 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @LinkedErrorMessage = NULL
					DELETE @InsertLinkedPrdTmpTbl

					INSERT INTO @InsertLinkedPrdTmpTbl
					EXEC [dbo].[MobspInsertDocItemFromLinkedPrd]
						@ProductId = @diPrdId,
						@DocId = @diDocId,
						@WHId = @diWHId,
						@UserId = @UserId,
						@FPId = @FPId,
						@Month1 = @Month1,
						@RegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
						@RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
						@AppType = @AppType,
						@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
						@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID,
						@PrdStockTxt = @PrdStockTxt

					SELECT TOP 1
						@LinkedErrorMessage = [ErrorMessage]
					FROM @InsertLinkedPrdTmpTbl

					IF(ISNULL(@LinkedErrorMessage, '') <> '')
					BEGIN
						SET @ResLinkedErrorMessage = @ResLinkedErrorMessage + '#|^|#' + 'InsertId=' + CAST(@diInsertedDocItemID AS NVARCHAR(10)) +
						 '|^|MSG=' + @LinkedErrorMessage
					END
				END


	
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrDocItem
		INTO @diInsertedDocItemID, @diDocItemID, @diDocId, @diPrdId, @diQuantity, @diCostId, @diCost,
				@diDescription, @diIsFix, @diWHId, @diNotVisible, @diFCR, @diFCRI, @diTracing, @diPrdUnitId,
				@diQuantityTotal, @diPrdUnitIdI, @diPrdUnitIRateResult, @diPrdNickname, @diInsertLinkedProducts
   
		END CLOSE CrsrDocItem DEALLOCATE CrsrDocItem

	END TRY
	BEGIN CATCH
		SET @ResErrorMessage = @ResErrorMessage + '#|^|#' + 'Error'
	END CATCH


	EndOfScript:
		PRINT 'EndOfScript:'

	IF((ISNULL(@ResErrorMessage, '') <> ''))
	BEGIN
		ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
		SET @ResInsertedDocItemIds = NULL
		SELECT 0
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		COMMIT TRANSACTION Main
		SET @ResInsertedDocItemIds = 
			(SELECT [DocItemID] AS [Id], [InsertedDocItemID] AS [InsertId] FROM @DocItemsTbl FOR JSON PATH)
		SELECT 1
	END

/**/
END







GO

/*AI991008- Add update company for mainpersonid if cmpmainpersonid is null*/
/*AI14000304 - preTel + Tel for checking duplicate instead of Tel*/
/*AI14010329 - User Mobsp instead of CRMsp for dup checks*/
/*AI14010621 - Use System datetime for FirstRegisterDate And RegisterDate*/
/*AI14011027 - Convert Arabic characters to Persian*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspInsertQuickPerson]
/*
	EXEC MobspInsertQuickPerson 
*/
(
@PersonList NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
@UserId INT,
@Year1 SMALLINT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@AppType VARCHAR(20), 
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL,
@TypeTable NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
/**/
@InsertedPersonIds NVARCHAR(1000) OUTPUT,
@ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** TRANSACTION *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/***************************** Define fields ************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
DECLARE @IsDuplicate BIT
/*RZ961206- Add time to date*/
DECLARE @RegisterTime NVARCHAR(8)
SET @RegisterTime = (CONVERT(CHAR(8), @RegisterDate, 108))
/************************************ Error *************************************/
DECLARE @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50), @ErrorNumber INT
/************** Set CantSaveDupPrsMob & CantSaveDupPrsEmail *********************/
DECLARE @CantSaveDupPrsMob BIT, @CantSaveDupPrsemail BIT, @CantSaveDupCmpCode BIT,@CantSaveDupTel BIT, @CantSaveDupCmpTelII BIT, @CantSaveDupCmpFax BIT, @CantSaveDupCmpZipCode BIT,
	 @CantSaveDupCmpNationalCode BIT, @CantSaveDupCmpEconomicCode BIT, @CantSaveDupCmpMobile BIT, @CantSaveDupPrsNationalCode BIT
SELECT TOP 1 
      @CantSaveDupPrsMob = [CantSaveDupPrsMob]
      ,@CantSaveDupPrsEmail =[CantSaveDupPrsEmail]
	  ,@CantSaveDupTel = [CantSaveDupTel]
	  ,@CantSaveDupCmpFax = [CantSaveDupFax]
	  ,@CantSaveDupCmpZipCode = [CantSaveDupZipCode]
	  ,@CantSaveDupCmpNationalCode = [CantSaveDupCmpNationalCode]
	  ,@CantSaveDupCmpEconomicCode = [CantSaveDupCmpEconomicCode]
	  ,@CantSaveDupPrsNationalCode = [CantSaveDupPrsNationalCode]
 FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting]
/************************ Make A Table For PersonList ***************************/
DECLARE @PersonTmpTable TABLE (
	[CompanyPersonID] INT,
	[prsCompanyId] INT,
	[prsLName] NVARCHAR(100),
	[prsFName] NVARCHAR(50),
	[prsTitleId] INT,
	[prsJobId] INT,
	[prsTypeId] INT,
	[prsGroupId] INT,
	[prsSexId] INT,
	[prsEducationId] INT,
	[prsPreTel] NVARCHAR(10),
	[prsTel] NVARCHAR(15),
	[prsTelUpTo] NVARCHAR(10),
	[prsTelDesc] NVARCHAR(50),
	[prsTelII] NVARCHAR(15),
	[prsFax] NVARCHAR(15),
	[prsFaxDesc] NVARCHAR(50),
	[prsMobile] NVARCHAR(15),
	[prsWebSite] NVARCHAR(50),
	[prsEmail] NVARCHAR(50),
	[prsEmailII] NVARCHAR(50),
	[prsCountryId] INT,
	[prsProvinceId] INT,
	[prsCityId] INT,
	[prsAddress] NVARCHAR(250),
	[prsAddressDesc] NVARCHAR(100),
	[prsZipCode] NVARCHAR(50),
	[prsPostBox] NVARCHAR(50),
	[prsNationalCode] NVARCHAR(30),
	[prsIdentityNumber] NVARCHAR(30),
	[prsFatherName] NVARCHAR(50),
	[prsBirthDate] NVARCHAR(8),
	[prsBirthDateTime] DATETIME,
	[prsBirthPlace] NVARCHAR(50),
	[prsIsMarried] BIT,
	[prsMarriageDate] NVARCHAR(8),
	[prsMarriageDateTime] DATETIME,
	[prsEducationField] NVARCHAR(50),
	[prsSkill] NVARCHAR(50),
	[prsStudyField] NVARCHAR(50),
	[prsInteresting] NVARCHAR(500),
	[prsSpecialPoint] NVARCHAR(500),
	[prsDescription] NVARCHAR(500),
	[prsImportDate] NVARCHAR(8),
	[prsImportDateTime] DATETIME,
	[prsDepartmentId] INT,
	[prsStopSendSms] BIT,
	[prsOrderView] INT,
	[prsWorkingOut] BIT,
	[CompanyPersonTemplateID] INT,
	[prsFirstRegisterUserId] INT,
	[prsFirstRegisterDatePersian] NVARCHAR(8),
	[prsTelegramId] INT,
	[prsFirstRegisterDate] DATETIME,
	[PersonPicture] VARBINARY(MAX)/*IMAGE*/
)
/********************************************************************************/
/***************************** Split fields *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
SET @IsDuplicate  = 0
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** Person ******************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRAN
/**/
DECLARE @PrsAllFields NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
DECLARE @PrsField NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
/**************** Define & set null field **************/
DECLARE 
	@CompanyPersonID INT = NULL,
	@prsCompanyId INT = NULL,
	@prsLName NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
	@prsFName NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@prsTitleId INT = NULL,
	@prsJobId INT = NULL,
	@prsTypeId INT = NULL,
	@prsGroupId INT = NULL,
	@prsSexId INT = NULL,
	@prsEducationId INT = NULL,
	@prsPreTel NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
	@prsTel NVARCHAR(15) = NULL,
	@prsTelUpTo NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
	@prsTelDesc NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@prsTelII NVARCHAR(15) = NULL,
	@prsFax NVARCHAR(15) = NULL,
	@prsFaxDesc NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@prsMobile NVARCHAR(15) = NULL,
	@prsWebSite NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@prsEmail NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@prsEmailII NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@prsCountryId INT = NULL,
	@prsProvinceId INT = NULL,
	@prsCityId INT = NULL,
	@prsAddress NVARCHAR(250) = NULL,
	@prsAddressDesc NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
	@prsZipCode NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@prsPostBox NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@prsNationalCode NVARCHAR(30) = NULL,
	@prsIdentityNumber NVARCHAR(30) = NULL,
	@prsFatherName NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@prsBirthDate NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@prsBirthDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
	@prsBirthPlace NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@prsIsMarried BIT = NULL,
	@prsMarriageDate NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@prsMarriageDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
	@prsEducationField NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@prsSkill NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@prsStudyField NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@prsInteresting NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
	@prsSpecialPoint NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
	@prsDescription NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
	@prsImportDate NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@prsImportDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
	@prsFirstRegisterUserId INT = NULL,
	@prsDepartmentId INT = NULL,
	@prsStopSendSms BIT = NULL,
	@prsOrderView INT = NULL,
	@prsWorkingOut BIT = NULL,
	@CompanyPersonTemplateID INT = NULL,
	@prsFirstRegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@prsTelegramId INT = NULL,
	@prsFirstRegisterDate DATETIME  = NULL,
	@PersonPicture VARBINARY(MAX)/*IMAGE*/ = NULL	
IF(@PersonList IS NOT NULL AND @PersonList <> '')
BEGIN
WHILE LEN(@PersonList) > 0 AND (@PersonList IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	/*@PersonList = N'prsName=name1|#|prsTitle=1|#||^#^|prsName=name2|#|prsTitle=2|#||^#^|'*/
    IF PATINDEX('%|^#^|%', @PersonList) > 0
    BEGIN
        SET @PrsAllFields = SUBSTRING(@PersonList,
                                    0,
                                    PATINDEX('%|^#^|%', @PersonList))
        /*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
        /*For example => @PrsAllFields = prsName=name1|#|prsTitle=1|#|*/
        /*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
        /*---------------------------- BEGIN Fill fields  --------------------------------*/
        /*BEGIN PERSON*/
        DECLARE @PAllFields NVARCHAR(MAX)
        SET @PAllFields = @PrsAllFields
        
        /****************  set null field **************/         
			SET @CompanyPersonID  = NULL
			SET @prsCompanyId  = NULL
			SET @prsLName  = NULL
			SET @prsFName  = NULL
			SET @prsTitleId  = NULL
			SET @prsJobId  = NULL
			SET @prsTypeId  = NULL
			SET @prsGroupId  = NULL
			SET @prsSexId  = NULL
			SET @prsEducationId  = NULL
			SET @prsPreTel  = NULL
			SET @prsTel  = NULL
			SET @prsTelUpTo  = NULL
			SET @prsTelDesc  = NULL
			SET @prsTelII  = NULL
			SET @prsFax  = NULL
			SET @prsFaxDesc  = NULL
			SET @prsMobile  = NULL
			SET @prsWebSite  = NULL
			SET @prsEmail  = NULL
			SET @prsEmailII  = NULL
			SET @prsCountryId  = NULL
			SET @prsProvinceId  = NULL
			SET @prsCityId  = NULL
			SET @prsAddress  = NULL
			SET @prsAddressDesc  = NULL
			SET @prsZipCode  = NULL
			SET @prsPostBox  = NULL
			SET @prsNationalCode  = NULL
			SET @prsIdentityNumber  = NULL
			SET @prsFatherName  = NULL
			SET @prsBirthDate  = NULL
			SET @prsBirthDateTime  = NULL
			SET @prsBirthPlace  = NULL
			SET @prsIsMarried  = NULL
			SET @prsMarriageDate  = NULL
			SET @prsMarriageDateTime  = NULL
			SET @prsEducationField  = NULL
			SET @prsSkill  = NULL
			SET @prsStudyField  = NULL
			SET @prsInteresting  = NULL
			SET @prsSpecialPoint  = NULL
			SET @prsDescription  = NULL
			SET @prsImportDate  = NULL
			SET @prsImportDateTime  = NULL
			SET @prsFirstRegisterUserId  = NULL
			SET @prsDepartmentId  = NULL
			SET @prsStopSendSms  = NULL
			SET @prsOrderView  = NULL
			SET @prsWorkingOut  = NULL
			SET @CompanyPersonTemplateID  = NULL
			SET @prsFirstRegisterDatePersian  = NULL
			SET @prsTelegramId  = NULL
			SET @prsFirstRegisterDate   = NULL
			SET @PersonPicture  = NULL				
                
        /**/
        WHILE LEN(@PAllFields) > 0
		BEGIN
			IF PATINDEX('%|#|%', @PAllFields) > 0
			BEGIN
				SET @PrsField = SUBSTRING(@PAllFields,
											0,
											PATINDEX('%|#|%', @PAllFields))
				/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
				/*Get parameter name and split value form parameter*/
				/*For example => prsTypeId=102 => prsTypeId= => prsTypeId => Compare => value*/
				DECLARE @PrsFieldValue NVARCHAR(MAX)
				DECLARE @PrsFieldName NVARCHAR(200)
				SET @PrsFieldName = SUBSTRING(@PrsField, 1, CHARINDEX('=',@PrsField)-1)                                    
				SET @PrsFieldValue = SUBSTRING(@PrsField, CHARINDEX('=', @PrsField)+1, Len(@PrsField))
				/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
				/*---------------------------- BEGIN Fill variabele ------------------------------*/
				/*BEGIN PERSON*/
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'CompanyPersonID'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @CompanyPersonID = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PrsFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsCompanyId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @prsCompanyId = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PrsFieldValue AS INT) END

					/*RZ971222- Add check access to cmp */
					IF(@prsCompanyId IS NOT NULL)
					BEGIN
						DECLARE @IsAccessCompanyId BIT = 0

						EXEC dbo.MobspCheckCmpDataPermissionForUser 	
						@Mode = 8,
						@Id = @prsCompanyId,
						@CrmUserId = @UserId,
						@IsAccess = @IsAccessCompanyId OUTPUT
				
						IF(@IsAccessCompanyId = 0)
						BEGIN
							DECLARE @CmpName NVARCHAR(2000)
							SET @CmpName = (SELECT
												CASE  WHEN (@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
													THEN (SELECT  
															ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId),'') + ' ' +
															ISNULL(cmpName,'')
													FROM dbo.CrmCompany C WHERE C.CompanyID = @prsCompanyId)
													ELSE (SELECT  
															ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId AND CT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'') + ' ' +
															ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CL WHERE CL.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CL.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')
														  FROM dbo.CrmCompany C WHERE C.CompanyID = @prsCompanyId)
												END
											 )
							
							SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + '|Msg=CompanyAccess|' + CAST(@CompanyPersonID AS NVARCHAR(10)) + '|PRSID|' + ISNULL(@CmpName,'') + '|Msg=CompanyAccess|'
						END	 
					END
					/*RZ971222- END*/

				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsLName'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsLName = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
					SET @prsLName = (SELECT dbo.[fnConvertArabicCharacterToPersian](@prsLName)) /*AI14011027*/
					/*IF(@prsLName IS NOT NULL)/*check duplicate*/
					BEGIN					
						DECLARE	@ResultCheckDuplicateForPrsLName INT = 0	
						EXEC [dbo].[MobspCheckDuplicateCompany]
								@Type  = 0, 
								@Id = 0, 
								@Val = @prsLName , 
								@TitleID = 0, 
								@CityId = 0, 
								@UserID  = @UserId ,							
								@Result = @ResultCheckDuplicateForPrsLName OUTPUT,
								/*RZ961129- Multi language*/
								@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
								@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID
						IF(@ResultCheckDuplicateForPrsLName > 0)
							SET @IsDuplicate = 1						
					END*/
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsFName'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsFName = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
					SET @prsFName = (SELECT dbo.[fnConvertArabicCharacterToPersian](@prsFName)) /*AI14011027*/
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsTitleId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @prsTitleId = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PrsFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsJobId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @prsJobId = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PrsFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsTypeId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @prsTypeId = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PrsFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsGroupId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @prsGroupId = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PrsFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsSexId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @prsSexId = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PrsFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsEducationId'
				BEGIN
					SELECT @prsEducationId = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@PrsFieldValue AS INT) END
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsPreTel'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsPreTel = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsTel'
				BEGIN
					SET @prsTel = CASE WHEN @PrsFieldValue = '' OR @PrsFieldValue = 'null' THEN NULL ELSE @PrsFieldValue END	
					IF(@prsTel IS NOT NULL)
					BEGIN
						DECLARE @HavePermissionDuplicatePrsTel INT
						IF EXISTS (
						SELECT * FROM dbo.CrmsysRollPermission
						WHERE FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmCompanyI' )
						AND FormActionId = (SELECT Id FROM dbo.CrmsysFormActions WHERE ActionName = N'actCanSaveTelDupAtTimeLimit'
											AND FormId = (SELECT ID FROM dbo.CrmsysForms WHERE FormName = N'frmCompanyI' ))
						AND RollId = (SELECT TOP 1 RollId FROM dbo.UsersPermission WHERE UserId = @UserId AND PrjId = 11/*CRM*/))
						SET @HavePermissionDuplicatePrsTel = 1
							ELSE
						SET @HavePermissionDuplicatePrsTel = 0

						IF(@CantSaveDupTel = 1 AND @HavePermissionDuplicatePrsTel = 0)
						BEGIN
							/*AI14000304 - preTel + Tel for checking duplicate instead of Tel*/
							DECLARE @chckPrsTel NVARCHAR(100)
							SET @chckPrsTel = ISNULL(@prsPreTel, '') + @prsTel

							DECLARE @ResultCheckDuplicateForPrsTel INT
							EXEC MobspCheckDuplicateTel @chckPrsTel,2, 0,@LanguageID,@DefaultLanguageID /*AI14010329*/
							SET @ResultCheckDuplicateForPrsTel = @@ROWCOUNT
							IF(@ResultCheckDuplicateForPrsTel > 0)
								SET @IsDuplicate = 1
						END
					END																	
				END
				/*--------------------------*/
				IF @PrsFieldName = 'prsTelUpTo
